# TODO archive

> **What this file is:** append-only history of completed work moved out of
> `TODO.md` by a periodic GC sweep (`make todo-gc`, see
> `.claude/skills/todo-md/gc.md` — feature PRs never write here, so this
> file never conflicts). Each item is one line, prefixed with its ancestor
> chain (`- [x] A > B > **C**: ...`), so a grep hit names the whole path.
> Wording and links into `docs/sketch/*`, issues, and PRs are kept verbatim;
> only the layout is normalized.
>
> **Reading this file:** do not read it whole — `rg` for a symbol or
> feature name; the matching line is the whole entry.
>
> Initial split: every `[x]` entry from `TODO.md`, plus the completed `[x]`
> children of still-open `[-]` parents, plus the verbatim status text of
> still-open items that was compacted out of `TODO.md`. Original order is
> preserved; items still open are marked **still open** and live in `TODO.md`.

## To Be Implemented (completed entries)

### `minigo`: Stack-VM Interpreter (redesign of go-scan's tree-walking `minigo`) ([docs/sketch/plan-minigo-vm.md](./docs/sketch/plan-minigo-vm.md))

Skeleton landed: lazy per-package loading, per-function compile, struct/method/closure/multi-return. Unsupported constructs compile to `OpTrap` instead of failing.

- [x] **`defer` / `recover` semantics**: defers run LIFO through the VM's frame teardown on both normal return and panic unwind; `recover()` is visible only inside deferred calls and can mutate named results. Script `*Panic` is recoverable, `*Trap` is not.
- [x] **`go`, `chan`, `select`**: documented single-thread approximation — `go f()` runs synchronously, channels are unbounded queues (sends never block), select picks the first ready case in source order. Operations that would block forever (recv on empty open channel, select without ready case or default) trap instead of deadlocking.
- [x] **Concurrency fuzz round** (thought-guided fuzzing over ~80 cases; [docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-concurrency.md](./docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-concurrency.md)): fixed — func-typed host params (`sync.Once.Do`) now adapt via `reflect.MakeFunc` on the caller's VM; empty-interface channel sends preserve pointer identity and pass slices/maps (send-side `toReflectValue` now passes runtime values through); bound `time.Duration` constants work (`intOf` case + `binaryOp` normalization — synctest's fake clock had hidden the bug); an empty `default:` body no longer turns a select into a deadlock; `for k, v := range ch` traps; procExit unwind drains remaining defers; bound-intrinsic callbacks (sort/slices) run on the calling VM (`sort.Slice` was also bound as an uncallable raw method value); concurrent `Engine.Call`/`EvalExpr` get a per-call VM (`-race` clean); `for i := 0; i < N; i++` loop vars are fresh per iteration (Go 1.22 semantics); `ImportRef.Materialize` and bootstrap panics record their error instead of leaving `nil`/`Initializing` behind. Documented limitation: a goroutine parked inside a non-select host call (`Wait.Wait`, `Mutex.Lock`) outlives its process (`TestHostParkLeak`).
- [x] **sync-package implementation round** (unit tests first, then a rebuilt ~/concfuzz corpus; [docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-concurrency.md](./docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-concurrency.md) §7): fixed — `sync.Map`/`Pool`/`Cond`/`Locker`/`NewCond`/`OnceFunc`/`OnceValue`/`OnceValues` bound (only WaitGroup/Mutex/RWMutex/Once existed); GoValue-boxed funcs are callable (`p.New()`); keyed host composite literals (`&sync.Pool{New: f}`) via `initHostLiteral` — which transitively unblocked `io` loading from GOROOT source (`blackHolePool`); foreign-goroutine `VM.Call` reroutes through `Spawn`+`Task.Wait` keyed by goroutine id, removing the documented adaptFunc hazard as a class (`wg.Go`, `Pool.New` from other goroutines — `runtime.Task.Result` added); embedded host-typed fields promote methods AND fields (`struct{ sync.Mutex }`, `struct{ sync.Pool }` — `findMethod` host branch + `promotedHostField`); `x.(*T)` on host pointer boxes matches by boxed native type (`c.L.(*sync.Mutex)`).
- [x] **sync-round leftovers** (unit tests first, language corpus re-run clean: 38 PASS / 4 PASS-REJECT; [docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-concurrency.md](./docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-concurrency.md) §8): fixed — `time.AfterFunc` bound via a raw `BuiltinFunc` keeping the caller VM — timer callbacks run through `vc.Spawn` like `go f()`, so a callback panic fails the process through the normal goroutine-failure path (not an unrecovered panic on the timer's host goroutine); ambiguous promoted members trap via `promotedField`'s BFS extended to host-typed embeds (`promotedHostField` deleted — `hostMemberInner` resolves at type level — `Type.FieldByName`'s index path and a recursive `hostMethodInner` also carry the host's own embed depth, so promoted-inside-host members (Template→*parse.Tree→Root) lose to shallower script members instead of trapping ambiguous; contributed types are visited once per path so a recursively-embedded host type (struct{ *T }) can't loop the depth walk into a stack overflow; `hostNilEmbed` feeds nilPaths) and to script method hits (`methHits` count toward ambiguity but dispatch stays in `findMethod`), so host+host, host+script, and script+script same-depth collisions all trap like Go's compile-time rejection; `VM.procDead` + a `done`-closed check in `Spawn` refuse spawns on a dead process, so a timer registered by a finished run never fires (was observable as a detached-proc callback — `TestAfterFuncDiesWithProc`); defined types over a host type (`type MyMutex sync.Mutex`) promote the underlying's fields but not its methods — method eligibility is checked on the unpeeled typedef (`hostMemberInner(..., methods)` + `raw.Methods`), matching Go's empty method set for defined types; nil callbacks register in `time.AfterFunc` and fail only at fire time through the goroutine-failure path (`call` panics on bare `runtime.Nil` like Go's nil call); through a nil `*T` host embed, pointer-receiver methods take the nil receiver (`hostNilCallable`) while fields and value-receiver methods still panic on the dereference.
- [x] **Promoted-method resolution gaps** (`findMethod` was DFS first-wins — [docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-concurrency.md](./docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-concurrency.md) §8.6): fixed — `promotedField` is now `promotedMember`, binding promoted methods and interface-stored receivers itself at BFS depth, and `findMethod`/`FindMethod`/`hostFieldName`/`structOf` are deleted. A shallower promoted method now beats a deeper one (`struct{X{A};B}` binds `B.M` like Go), interface embeds and method-vs-iface collisions count toward ambiguity, a missing member on a recursive embed (`struct{*S}`) traps instead of overflowing the stack, and a nil `*T` embed binds a pointer receiver's nil (deep chains panic on the receiver's own dereference, like Go). Pinned by `TestPromotedMethodResolution` in testdata/methoddepth.
- [x] **Language-surface fuzz round** (thought-guided differential fuzzing over ~40 programs vs `go run`; [docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-language.md](./docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-language.md)): fixed — elided-key struct literals in maps compile against `MapType.Key`; compound assign peels `ParenExpr`; package-var init order is dependency-driven DFS (was a flat Kahn that ran unrelated vars early); anonymous structs with equal field lists compare equal; map keys carry a structural type tag so separately-written `struct{A int}`/`[2]int` keys match and slice keys panic unhashable; `a[:]` on an array yields a `[]Elem` slice typedef (cap/sharing no longer broken); sized-int decls tag the value so unary/binary ops re-mask the width (`-uint8(5)` is 251, named aliases like `MyU8` mask through the underlying); `(*[N]int)(s)`/`[N]int(s)` slice→array conversions work; function-local `type` decls feed generic inference via element evidence + frame-local typedef scan; `fmt.Errorf` `%w` builds a real wrap chain (`errors.Is`/`Unwrap`/`As` walk it); `%T` rewrites to the script type spelling (host fmt bypasses `Formatter` for it); `%q`/`%s`/`%x` on `[]byte` and elementwise scalar verbs (`%c` on `[]rune`) render like Go; `print`/`println` write stderr; recover values print `runtime error:` prefixes, assert/index/nil-call messages match Go's wording; `&m[k]` traps at run time (nearest loud fail for Go's compile rejection). Designed limitations recorded as lim-* cases — all lifted in the leftovers round (complex numbers, 3-index slices, arbitrary-precision consts).
- [x] **Fuzz-round leftovers** (all resolved; [docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-language.md](./docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-language.md) §7 — language corpus 40 PASS + 4 PASS-REJECT, no DIFF/TRAP/ACCEPT): `runtime.UConst` holds `go/constant` lazily through const cells, folded constant expressions, and mixed const/literal ops (`constOf` lifts bare int64/float64 back) — `const B = 1<<100` compiles unused, `var x = 1<<100` / `var f = 1e500` trap `overflows int`/`overflows float64` at the bind (`lim-bigconst`); char literals are UConst with rune flavor so `'a'` prints `int32` and propagates through `'a'+1`; `complex64`/`complex128` ride `*runtime.GoValue{V: complex64|complex128}` (the Go width IS the type; declared complex types still tag `Named`) with `complex`/`real`/`imag` bound — `real`/`imag` keep constants in the constant domain (`constant.Real/Imag`), mixed-width ops trap `mismatched types complex64 and complex128`, ordered comparisons trap `complex numbers are not ordered` (`lim-complex`); three-index slicing `s[a:b:c]` via `OpSlice` B=1 popping a capacity operand (strings reject) (`lim-threeidx`); assert failures name the static interface type (`OpLocalTyp`/`OpUpvalTyp`/`OpGlobalTyp` push the operand's static typedef → `interface conversion: main.asI is main.asT, not main.asT2` plus missing-method form); `errors.As` resolves the declared typedef via `sameErrTyp` (Kind+Name+Pkg.Path) so same-named types in different packages can't collide; float32 slots narrow via `float64(float32(x))` at coerce/convert/binaryOp/display; `[]byte`/`[]rune` element reads carry the element typedef (`b[0]` prints `uint8` under `%T`); `%T` of `int64(5)` prints `int64` and `'a'` `int32` (conversions tag the target typedef via `materializeConstErr`, `scriptTypeString`/`typeNameOf` report a `UConst` by `DefaultName`).
- [x] **Use-case fuzz round** (28 realistic programs diffed against `go run` as oracle — text/scripting bias; [docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-usecase.md](./docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-usecase.md)): fixed — `json.Unmarshal(data, &v)` two-arg form with tag-driven `jsonShape` decode (+ `VMCaller.ElemZero`); `json:` tags honored on marshal AND unmarshal (`StructFieldTags` populated at all four typedef sites); struct marshal emits fields in declaration order (`orderedObject`) with `omitempty`; `errors.As` works on script error types (`hostErrOf`/`scriptError` chain walk, pointer-valued targets via inner Cell, interface targets match any error); `errors.Join` skips nil; `strings.Builder`/`NewReplacer`/`TrimFunc`/`IndexFunc`/`Map` bound (runePred callback adapter); `fmt.Fprint*` bound with `asWriter` unwrapping `&b`; `time.ParseDuration` + duration methods/arithmetic; uint64 literals >MaxInt64 box as `GoValue{uint64}` with wrap-around arithmetic and `uint64(x)`/`%x` unsigned rendering; nil `[]string`/`[]byte` host results become `TypedNil` (`m == nil` works); unnamed host slices/arrays unbox element-wise (regexp `[]int` results indexable); `os.Args` is a variable (flag's init reads it); function-local `type` decls resolve element types at runtime (`localElemTypedef` fallback at all `ElemOf` sites); named-const indices in `[]T{Const: v}` literals; map output sorts keys (fmtsort); `os.DirEntry`/`FileInfo` marker interfaces for `filepath.WalkDir` callbacks.

- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > Unsigned shift semantics: `binaryOp` routes `<<`/`>>` through `shiftOp` with unsigned-count bit reinterpretation and sign-fill `>>`; `^`/`-` and `/`/`%` on `uint`/`uint64`/`uintptr` run in the unsigned domain (lim-sha). `x << uint(-4)` is 0; `x >> -1` panics `negative shift amount`.

- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > `net/http`: interpreted from GOROOT source end-to-end — a real loopback server+client roundtrip answers `200 OK` (lim-http + `testdata/httpio` `TestNetHTTPRoundtrip`). - [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > `gopkg.in/yaml.v3` external module: the `unsafe.Pointer` inside `internal/abi.TypeOf` was bypassed by delegating `reflect` to a native facade (`minigo/minireflect`, bound via `minigo_reflect.go`) — `Unmarshal`/`Marshal` both work, and the same facade carries `BurntSushi/toml` decode, `text/template` source interpretation, and `net/url` source interpretation ([docs/sketch/ja/experiment-yaml-reflect.md](./docs/sketch/ja/experiment-yaml-reflect.md); [#40](https://github.com/podhmo/minigo/issues/40)).
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > `fmt` should unwrap facade reflect.Values before formatting — `fmt.Println(reflect.ValueOf([]int{1,2,3}))` prints `<[]int Value>` where Go prints `[1 2 3]` (Go's fmt detects reflect.Value args and formats the underlying value; found via minigo-usecasefuzz `reflectdeep`). Fixed — `fmtArg` unwraps a facade `*RValue` exactly once via the new `RValue.Unwrap` (invalid → `<invalid reflect.Value>`, a Value payload stays wrapped for its `<T Value>` String, host payloads go native); `%T` spells `reflect.Value`/`*reflect.rtype`, `%p` emits `%!p(reflect.Value=<underlying %v>)` (pinned: `reflect_fmt-value-unwrap`).
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > `reflect.TypeAssert[T]` — Go 1.25's generic type-assert builtin needs explicit type args on a bound builtin (`F[T](x)` instantiation over `*runtime.BuiltinFunc`); was the blocker on encoding/xml's decode path (lim-xml trapped inside `Decoder.unmarshal` at `TypeAssert[Unmarshaler]`). Fixed — `reflect.TypeAssert` binds via `GenFn`: zero Value panics `call of reflect.TypeAssert on zero Value`, read-only Value panics `cannot return value obtained from unexported field or method`, interface-typed v asserts its dynamic payload (nil iface → !ok), interface T checks `Implements`, concrete T checks exact type identity; !ok returns T's zero (pinned: `reflect_type-assert`).

- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g1 field addressability — unaddressable `Field` reports `CanAddr`/`CanSet` true; `MapIndex` struct fields accept `SetInt` instead of panicking (pinned: `reflect_field-addressability`, `reflect_map-read-copy`). - [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g2 anonymous type canonical keys — `TypeOf([]int{}) != SliceOf(TypeOf(0))` (pinned: `reflect_slice-type-identity`). - [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g3 `MakeSlice` capacity + len/cap validation (pinned: `reflect_make-slice-capacity`, `reflect_make-slice-invalid`). - [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g4 `IsZero` on arrays, nil composites, invalid `Value` (pinned: `reflect_is-zero`, `reflect_zero-invalid`). - [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g5 `SetInt`/`SetUint` narrowing to the declared width (pinned: `reflect_set-int-width`, `reflect_set-uint-width`). Fixed — `declTd` resolves the ref's declared typedef and `truncInt` masks/sign-extends to it (int8 cell stores int8(257) == 1). (PR: validation boundaries)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g6 type aliases share the target's identity — `TypeFor[byte]() == TypeFor[uint8]()` (pinned: `reflect_alias-identity`). Fixed — `keyOf` resolves `KindAlias` through `AliasOf` and folds predeclared `byte`→`uint8`/`rune`→`int32`. (PR: type identity)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g7 local + instantiated named types keep distinct identity — `S[int]` vs `S[string]` (pinned: `reflect_generic-identity`, `reflect_local-type-identity`). Fixed — `TypeDef.Local` marks function-local declarations (keyed by their `Spec` declaration object) and type args append a `[arg]` suffix to the canonical name. (PR: type identity)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g8 host numeric boxing loses identity — `any(time.Weekday(4)) == any(int(4))` regressed to true; `reflect.Int+1` lost its `reflect.Kind` tag (pinned: `reflect_host-numeric-regression`; MREs: host-numeric-equality, host-numeric-type). Fixed — `binaryOp` evaluates `==`/`!=` on the boxed operands so `eqlValue` sees dynamic types, and re-boxes int results into the unwrapped operand's named host type (`numTag`; mismatched named ints trap).
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g9 `NewSession` does not inherit `WithPackageModes` — a denied package imports fine from a child session (host-side MRE: session-policy). Fixed: `NewSession` now copies `pkgModes` (and `args`, the same class of dropped option).
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g10 `Indirect` must dereference a pointer once, not loop (pinned: `reflect_indirect-depth`). Fixed — `Indirect` calls `Elem` exactly once like Go (`**T` yields `*T`, not `T`). (PR: remaining minireflect edges)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g11 `ValueOf` must reflect a `reflect.Value` itself as a struct (pinned: `reflect_value-of-value`). (PR: remaining minireflect edges)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g12 `ValueOf` must snapshot a struct argument (pinned: `reflect_valueof-struct-copy`). Fixed — `valueOfValue` stores `runtime.Copy(v)`. (PR: copy boundaries)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g13 read-only provenance lost through `Elem`/`Slice` on unexported fields (pinned: `reflect_private-pointer`, `reflect_private-index`, `reflect_private-bytes`). - [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g16 scalar accessors/`Field` must panic on wrong kinds — `Int().Uint()`, `IsNil` on non-nillable (pinned: `reflect_primitive-isnil`, `reflect_get-kind-check`, `reflect_set-kind-check`, `reflect_pointer-field`). - [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g19 `Append` must reuse available backing capacity (pinned: `reflect_append_backing`). Fixed — `append(sl.Elems, ...)` reuses cap instead of a fresh backing. (PR: copy boundaries)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g20 `SetMapIndex` must copy struct values (pinned: `reflect_map-write-copy`). Fixed — value copies via `ifaceVal` (`runtime.Copy`). (PR: copy boundaries)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g21 `Send` must copy struct values (pinned: `reflect_send-copy`). Fixed — `ifaceVal` (`runtime.Copy`) on send; `Recv`/`TryRecv` copy on the way out. (PR: copy boundaries)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g22 `Call` must return zero values for a void function (pinned: `reflect_call-void`). Fixed — `callOut` consults the declared signature's result count via `callSig` (Function/Closure/BoundMethod decl or the typedef's FuncType): zero results yield an empty slice even though the VM returns NIL. - [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g23 `CallSlice` must expand the variadic tail (pinned: `reflect_call-slice`). (PR: call semantics)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g24 `Comparable` must recurse into structs and arrays (pinned: `reflect_comparable-struct`). Fixed — `comparableTd` recurses through field types and composite elements (struct {X []int} and [1][]int both report false). (PR: relations + conversions)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g25 `AssignableTo` named→unnamed underlying compatibility (pinned: `reflect_assignable-underlying`). Fixed — identical `TypUnderlyingSpelling` plus at least one unnamed side. (PR: relations + conversions)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g26 `ConvertibleTo` must reject invalid conversions (pinned: `reflect_convertible-invalid`). Fixed — reflect's own rules: identical underlying, non-complex numerics between themselves, complex only to complex, int<->string (rune), []byte/[]rune<->string, slice->array; `int -> complex` and `string -> []int` now report false. (PR: relations + conversions)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g27 `Equal` must use Go comparable-value equality (pinned: `reflect_equal-uncomparable`, `reflect_equal-named`, `reflect_equal-types`). Fixed — uncomparable types panic `comparing uncomparable type`, mismatched types report false, and `valueEqual` unwraps `Named` on both sides so N(1)==N(1). (PR: relations + conversions)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g28 `Bytes` must return a shared view of the slice (pinned: `reflect_bytes-alias`, `reflect_private-bytes`). Fixed — `Bytes` returns a `*runtime.Slice` aliasing the original `Elems` (like Go's Bytes the read-only flag does not apply), instead of a copied `[]byte` the write-through could never reach. (PR: read-only provenance)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g29 synthesized map/array metadata lost through value construction (pinned: `reflect_array-constructor`, `reflect_map-constructor`). Fixed — `ArrayOf`/`MapOf` carry `Anon` spellings and intern under the canonical `anon:` key, so constructed values resolve the same RType (map keys resolve through `keyTdOf`). (PR: type identity)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g30 channel direction must remain part of the type (pinned: `reflect_chan-direction`, `reflect_chan-type-direction`). Fixed — `TypSpelling` renders `ChanType.Dir` while `TypUnderlyingSpelling` erases it (direction is identity, not underlying); `ChanOf` builds the matching `Anon`. (PR: type identity)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g31 function dynamic types must retain their signature (pinned: `reflect_function-identity`). Fixed — `typeOfValue` spells a function's declared `FuncType` as its `Anon`. (PR: type identity)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g32 `Implements` must check signatures and require an interface target (pinned: `reflect_interface-signature`, `reflect_interface-noninterface`). Fixed — `Implements` compares method signatures (declared `FuncType` spelled under each member's own package/binds context) and panics `non-interface type passed to Type.Implements` on a non-interface target. (PR: member model)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g33 method sets must distinguish receivers and visibility (pinned: `reflect_method-pointer-set`; MRE: method-export-filter). Fixed — the new `MethodSet` hook collects declared + promoted method functions under Go's receiver rule (pointer-receiver members join only through a pointer type or embedded pointer field); `NumMethod`/`Method`/`MethodByName` expose exported members only. (PR: member model)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g34 method metadata must include `Type` and correct `PkgPath` (pinned: `reflect_method-metadata`). Fixed — `Method.Type` is a `func(T)+sig` RType built from the member's declared `FuncType` with the receiver prepended as the first parameter (as Go reports it). (PR: member model)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g35 `Convert` to string must convert, not format (pinned: `reflect_convert-string`, `reflect_convert-integer-string`). Fixed — a slice source decodes bytes to a string and an integer source yields a one-rune string instead of the `<[]uint8 Value>`/`<int Value>` descriptors. (PR: relations + conversions)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g36 `FieldByName` must traverse embedded fields, including pointers (pinned: `reflect_field-promotion`, `reflect_field-type-embedded-pointer`). Fixed — both the type- and value-side `FieldByName` recurse through embedded fields and peel pointer embeds. (PR: member model)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g37 unexported anonymous fields must retain `PkgPath` (pinned: `reflect_private-struct-name`). Fixed — `Field` reports `PkgPath` for unexported names, embedded or not. (PR: member model)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g38 `MapIter` must skip deleted keys (pinned: `reflect_map-iter-delete`). Fixed — `Next` consults the live map for each snapshot key, so a key deleted mid-iteration is skipped (added keys stay invisible — Go leaves that unspecified). (PR: remaining minireflect edges)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g39 slicing an array must produce a slice type (pinned: `reflect_array-slice-type`). Fixed — `Slice` on an array produces a `[]T` typedef carrying `Anon: &ast.ArrayType{Elt}` so it interns to the same RType as a script `[]T` literal. (PR: ref-view model)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g40 promoted `FieldRef` lookup must prefer the shallowest match (pinned: `reflect_promoted-depth`). Fixed — `FieldRef.Get`/`Set` now walk embedded fields breadth-first like `promotedField` (same-depth duplicates panic as ambiguous) instead of depth-first first-match, so `&s.X` resolves the same field `s.X` does. (PR: promoted depth)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g41 `ArrayOf` must reject negative lengths (pinned: `reflect_array-of-invalid`). Fixed — `n < 0` panics `reflect: negative length`. (PR: validation boundaries)
- [x] Use-case-fuzz leftovers > reflect-facade review findings > g42 array `Index`/`Slice` must retain addressability restrictions (pinned: `reflect_array-index-address`, `reflect_array-unaddressable-slice`). Fixed — array `Index` refs exist only when `v.ref != nil`; `Slice` traps `slice of unaddressable array`. (PR: ref-view model)

- [ ] **difffuzz harness** (`tools/difffuzz`: generated probes + batched shrinking + `$GOROOT/test` corpus, verdicts per minigo's trap contract; [docs/sketch/ja/difffuzz-harness.md](./docs/sketch/ja/difffuzz-harness.md); fix loop: `.claude/skills/difffuzz`). Landed: text/num domains, metamorphic contexts, symptom-preserving shrink, `-mask`, `-emit` → `testdata/difffuzz` xfail regressions (`TestDiffRegressions`). Findings below are pinned as `PENDING` cases where marked.
- [x] difffuzz harness > nil slice / nil map print as `<nil>` instead of `[]` / `map[]`: a typed nil renders its empty composite under every verb (`[]byte` nils render as `""`), `%#v` uses the `T(nil)` conversion form, `%p`/`%T` rewrite the spec to the script spelling (`0x0` for nil), verbs descend elementwise through composites (`%q`/`%s` on `[]int`, `%!s(int=N)` markers spell the declared element type), host-unboxed slices/arrays carry their element typedef, and `append(nil, empty...)` stays nil (`text_value_05d448e7`, `text_value_c971b474`, `text_value_17998073`, `text_value_3613fc16`, `text_value_appendnil` + `text_type_1ef86e37`, `text_type_3ca140c9`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > `fmt.Sprint(f())` with a multi-value call inserted spaces between string operands — the Tuple now spreads into individual host fmt operands (`fmtArgs`), so Go's neither-is-a-string rule sees the real scalars (`fmt.Sprint(strings.Cut("a=b", "="))` is `abtrue`) (`text_value_650e3d07`, `text_value_6d405433`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > A panic inside a script callback passed to a host function (`strings.Map`, `strings.TrimFunc`, `sort.*`, `slices.*Func`, `filepath.WalkDir`) surfaced as an unrecoverable `runtime trap: panic: …` — `v.Call` delivers script panics as errors across its boundary, so callback sites now re-raise via `callOrPanic` (matching `adaptFunc`/`syncOnceWrap`) and the panic reaches `recover()` like Go's (`text_panic-became-trap_b6ee4829`, `text_panic-became-trap_db893a51`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > `slices.Clone(nil)` returned a non-nil slice, and both `slices.Clone`/`maps.Clone` dropped the declared typedef — they now run as builtins returning the same typed nil and cloning with `Typ` preserved (so `maps.Clone(m)[k]++` zero-starts and `sort.Strings(clone)` keeps `[]string`); `sortInPlace` also no-ops on a nil slice like Go (`text_value_f50116fd`, word-count `maps.Clone(m); c[k]++` shapes).
- [x] difffuzz harness > `%T` differences: `rune`/`byte` spell `int32`/`uint8` (alias map + `typedefSpelling`), `s[i]` indexes to `uint8`, and host sized-int results keep their width — `scriptVal`/`goValueOf` box `int8`-`int32`/`uint`-`uintptr` results as `Named` instead of flattening to `int64`, so `unicode.ToUpper(r)` and `utf8.DecodeRune` spell `int32` (`text_type_sizedhost`). Host `[]string` results (`strings.Fields`, `regexp.FindAllString`) no longer print `interface{}` — unboxed host slices carry their element typedef (`text_type_1ef86e37`, `text_type_3ca140c9`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > `delete(m, k)` left the key in the map's insertion-order list — rendering and range showed `k:<nil>` after deletion (the `deleteKey(wordCount)` hunt shape `map[a:<nil>]`); the key now drops from `Order` with `Pairs` (`text_value_deletemap`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > `string(nilBytes)` / `string(nilRunes)` trapped `cannot convert *runtime.TypedNil to string` — the `string` conversion accepts a slice-typed nil as `""` like Go (`text_value_stringnil`). `maps.Clone` shapes were fixed by the Typ-preserving clone above.
- [x] difffuzz harness > slice bounds on a nil slice panicked with a bare `slice bounds out of range` — the TypedNil branch now renders Go's boundsError text (`[l:0]`, `[l:]`, `[:h] with capacity 0`, `[::m] with capacity 0`, `[:h:m]`); live slices already panic inside Go's indexing with the full message (`text_panic-message_66b1011f`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > Typed var decls from untyped consts lose the type: `var f float64 = 3` is an `int` and `f/2 == 1`; `var x int64 = 0` prints `int` — `coerceConcrete` now converts an int64 operand to float32/float64 and tags `int64`/`float32` decls as `Named` (zero-init too, via `wrapZero`), so every assignment-like site (decl/`=`/field/element/arg/return/send/`*p=`) lands converted; `e.Call` unwraps `Named` at the host boundary ([#35](https://github.com/podhmo/minigo/issues/35), `num_value_issue35`).
- difffuzz harness > _The entry below is the verbatim completed-work log of the still-open `[ ]` `$GOROOT/test` corpus sweep — the open remainder lives in `TODO.md`:_
- [ ] difffuzz harness > `$GOROOT/test` corpus sweep: the tracked files (typeswitch1/reorder/range/divmod/copy/switch) all PASS byte-identical — pick the next batch of SILENT/CRASH/HANG corpus programs on the next hunt. `switch.go` needed interface-tag case strictness — the compiler tracks interface-typed declarations (`var x any`, `x := any(v)`, iface params) and emits `BinEqlIface` pair-equality (`case 1:` never matches `any(float64(1.0))`, `interface{}(F(3))` keeps its `main.F` pair); a positional `default:` (fallthrough into following clauses); and `os.Exit` as a real `ExitRequest` process exit (no defers, no recover, `exit status N`). `typeswitch1.go`, `reorder.go`, `range.go`, `divmod.go`, `copy.go` PASS; `copy.go` needed an untyped const operand adopting the other operand's named type (`'a'+i%26` on `uint16` — `text_pass_constadopt`) and `arrayLen` resolving const names through globals + evaluating constant length exprs (`[N]T`, `[N*2]T` — `text_pass_arraylen`); `reorder.go` needed Go's two-phase assignment: LHS refs resolve before the RHS via new `OpSetRefs`/`OpUpvalRef` (`text_pass_reorder`), `return pair()` spreading into N named result slots (`text_pass_namedret`), `range.go` needed `=` range targets as per-iteration two-phase assigns (`OpSetRefs` B=1: iter pair pushes first, refs still see pre-iteration operands — `for i, x[i] =` binds through the old i, `*getvar(&x)` runs once per live iteration), uint `==`/`!=`, and const-domain comparisons returning a real `bool` not `constant.Value` (`text_pass_range`/`text_pass_uintcmp`/`text_pass_constcmp`). Done: `goprint.go` — `println`/`print` of a nil pointer-ish value now spells `0x0` (`[0/0]0x0` for a nil slice) like the runtime's printany; `typeswitch1.go` (PASS); `makeslice.go` — `make([]T, n)` checks len/cap before the host allocation so absurd lengths panic `makeslice: len out of range`/`cap out of range` instead of OOMing the VM, runtime-error panics now recover as `error` values (`runtime.RuntimeError` boxed through `Recover()`, so `err.(error).Error()` works like Go), and `unsafe.Sizeof` reads a pointer nil as 8 (was the interface pair 16 — switched `makeslice.go` onto its 64-bit branch) plus real sizes for named basics (`rune`→4, `byte`→1); `initialize.go` — mixed keyed/positional composite literals (`[16]byte{4: 1, 1, ...}`) no longer crash the compiler (positional elements ride an `ImplicitIndex` sentinel resolved to the running index), and keyed slice gaps fill with the element zero instead of `<nil>` — the remaining failure is the `reflect.DeepEqual`/`unsafe.Pointer` boundary from #40 (`text_value_mixedlit`). `map.go` — named map/array types in keyed literals resolve their underlying shape so `M{i: i+1}` reads `i` as an expression not a field name (`typeSpec` scope walker + `isKeyedLitShape`), `math.Float32bits`/`Float64bits`/`Float32frombits`/`Float64frombits` bound, host float32 values keep their `float32` tag (Named, float64 payload) so `-0 == +0` holds, and NaN map keys store under per-insert nonces — readable never, enumerable like Go (`Map.Keys` carries the canonical key per order slot) (`text_pass_mapfloatkey`). `nil.go` — a nil `*[N]T` behaves like Go: `len`/`cap` fold to N (`nilArrLen` via `VMCaller.ArrayLenOf`), index-only `for i := range p` yields 0..N-1 (an `i` iterator flagged `NilArr`), while the element read (`for i, v := range p`, `p[i]`) panics with a nil-pointer dereference (`text_pass_nilarrayptr`). `recover2.go` — assert failures panic with a `GoValue{error}` payload so `recover().(error).Error()` works like Go's TypeAssertionError; uncomparable checks now fire on the TYPE not the value (a nil slice field inside `T{[]int}` still panics `comparing uncomparable type main.T` on `z != z`, and hashing it names the outer type — `uncomparableValue`/`unhashableKey` cover slice/map/func live values and their typed/iface nils), and panic text spells package names (`main.T`), `interface {}`, and `int` like Go (`text_pass_recover2`). `struct0.go` — anonymous struct assert targets match by shape: `shapeSpelling` now renders `struct{` fields/tags, `interface{` methods, `func(` signatures and `[N]` array lengths precisely instead of collapsing every anon type to one bucket, so `interface{}(struct{}{}).(struct{})` asserts and `convShapeEq` stops overmatching (`text_pass_struct0`). `print.go` — `println` of a nil interface spells `(0x0,0x0)` like the runtime's printeface (typed-nil iface values too), and a host-boxed complex prints bare `(9+10i)` not `&{...}` (`text_pass_printiface`). `defernil.go` — `defer nilf()` registers fine and panics at the deferred call's invocation with a nil-pointer dereference (`invokeDeferred` maps a nil callee to a script `runtime.Panic`, like `v.call` already did) (`text_pass_defernil`). `devirtualization_nil_panics.go` — a method call on a nil interface now panics `invalid memory address or nil pointer dereference` like Go (`memberOf`/`memberOfType` map a nil receiver to a script `runtime.Panic`, so `recover()` inside the deferred func sees it); funclits get Go symbol names (`main.main.func1` via a `symName`/`litCount` in the compiler); `runtime.Callers`/`runtime.CallersFrames` bound through `VMCaller.CallerPCs`/`CallerFrame` — the VM snapshots live frames plus frames the in-flight panic already unwound (`v.unwinding`, cleared when the panic dies), so a deferred recover still sees the panicking frame — and `OpSelect` records the `.Sel` token position so `v.\n\tA()` reports A's line (`text_pass_nilpanic`). `const4.go` — `len(x[i])`/`cap(x[i])` folds to a constant when x's element typedef is an array, like Go: the compiler emits base + `OpLenIdxFold` + (index, OpIndex, OpCall), and the op skips the emitted index run — replacing the [callee, base] stack pair with the folded length — when the element typedef turns out to be `ast.ArrayType` (covers `len(s[10])` on a nil `[][30]int`, `len(m[""])` on `map[string][20]int`; non-array elements still evaluate the index and panic normally) (`text_pass_constlenidx`). `const3.go` — an empty const spec inherits the previous spec's TYPE too, not just its values: `Decl.InheritedType` carries it through the index, so `B` under `A T = e` coerces to `T` (`OpCoerceTop`) and `fmt.Sprintf("%v")` hits `T.String()` (`text_pass_constinherit`). `decl.go` — the function body's outermost block IS the signature's scope: params and named results now sit beside top-level `:=` names (the compiler runs the body's statements in the same fscope block instead of pushing one for the BlockStmt), so `a, b, s := f()` in `func x() (s string)` assigns to the result var, and a bare `return` yields "3" — same change for function literals (`text_pass_declredecl`). `zerosize.go` — zero-size pointees share `runtime.zerobase` like Go: `eqlValue`'s Cell arm compares `new([0]int)` cells equal when both elements are zero-size (`zeroSizeValue` — empty `struct{}`, `[0]T` array, or a struct of only zero-size fields), so `p == q`, `&x == p`, `&y == p` all hold (`text_pass_zerosize`). `bigalg.go` — `&a[0]` compares by backing array, not ref node: `eqlValue` gained an `IndexRef` arm (`refBase` unwraps Cell/Named to the container object; same base + same index = same address), so a slice stored in a map shares its element addresses through the copy (`text_pass_bigalg`). `recover3.go`/`zerodivide.go`/`gcstring.go`/`init1.go`/`winbatch.go`/`const7.go`/`heapsampling.go` — bound the missing `runtime` surface: `Compiler`, `GOROOT`, `Breakpoint` (no-op), `SetFinalizer` (registered, never fires — no GC), `KeepAlive`, `MemProfile`/`MemProfileRecord`, `MemProfileRate` as an assignable package-level cell (`setField` gained an `ImportRef` arm so `runtime.MemProfileRate = 1` writes through), `Error` as an interface typedef matching `RuntimeError` asserts, and `MemStats`/`ReadMemStats` backed by a real host `*runtime.MemStats`; `bytes.Buffer` is a hostType too so `var buf bytes.Buffer` boxes `*bytes.Buffer` (`text_pass_runtimebinds`). `const8.go` — local const blocks now back `iota` with a hidden local slot set to the spec index before each spec compiles (`fscope.iotaSlot` — declared lazily so a `const iota = iota` shadows it like any local, giving `B`/`C`/`Z` the declared const's value of 1), and an empty local const spec repeats the previous non-empty spec's expressions AND type (`text_pass_constiota`). `append.go` — `append([]byte, s...)` spreads the string's bytes (the only legal string spread), a materialized constant arg converts through the declared element type (`append([]complex128{}, 0)` stores `0+0i` — `typeOfValue` gains a `GoValue` arm so `TypeOf(ElemZero)` resolves `complex128`), and `reflect.DeepEqual` is bound to a script-side deep equal (`deepEql`: nil-ness + type identity honored, slices/maps/structs recurse, anonymous struct defs compare by shape so two `struct{}` sites are the same type) (`text_pass_spreadstr`). `intcvt.go`/`const.go`/`shift3.go` — untyped constants stay `UConst` through decl storage (`constExpr` emits `OpConst &runtime.UConst` when the spec's expression is constant-foldable) so each use materializes for its context (`const c1e3 = 1e3` assigns to `int` AND `float64`); comparisons convert the const to the operand's scalar type before comparing (`f == 1e100-1` rounds into float64 — `scalarConst`/`isCompareOp`); float/complex constants read through `constant.ToInt` where Go requires integers (recover-guarded `toIntConst` — `constant.Int64Val`/`Uint64Val` panic on non-Int kinds); `math.Max*` binds (`MaxUint`/`MaxUint64`/`MaxUintptr` as `UConst`, the sized ones as `int64`); non-const shifts read a `UConst` count as an unsigned integer without materializing (`x << (math.MaxUint+0)` clamps to 0, `x<<1.`/`x<<(1+0i)` count too), while both-const shifts fold in the exact domain (`chuge>>100` = 1 — `constShift`), and `arrayLen` reads `UConst` globals (`[N]T` with `const N = 5` — `lenConstInt`) (`text_pass_constdom`). `convT2X.go` — `==`/`!=` between differently-typed dynamic values is lawful: `any(u64) != any(u32)` returns true instead of a "mismatched types" trap (ordered ops and arithmetic still trap like Go) (`text_pass_namedcmp`). `rename.go` — predeclared names are redeclarable: `nil`/`true`/`false` now resolve through `declared` (fscope, generic binds, or a package-level index decl) before falling back to the builtin literal (`text_pass_predecl`). `simassign.go` — a lone call argument spreads its result tuple into the callee's params (`swap(swap(a, b))` — `callSpread` B=2, `popArgs` mode 2 expanding `*runtime.Tuple`; `OpDefer`/`OpGo` share it) (`text_pass_spreadcall`). `ddd.go`/`method7.go`/`method3.go` — method expressions `T.M`/`(*T).M`/`I.m` produce function values: declared methods bind the receiver param (pointer receivers take the Cell argument, value receivers ride `methodExprDeref`), and promoted/interface-required names return a `methodExprThunk` re-selecting the member on the actual receiver like Go's selector lowering; anonymous type expressions in value position (`interface{ m() }.m`) compile the typedef (`text_pass_methodexpr`). `method.go`/`method5.go` — a promoted value method through a nil embedded pointer panics at member selection like Go (`findMethod` descends through the nil `*T` field on the bare typedef — the method set lives on the type — and `structMember` raises the nil-deref panic when the receiver path can't deref; covers `var t1 T1; f = t1.M` binding-time panics) (`text_pass_nilembed`); `p = nil` on a pointer var keeps the inferred type (`assignCell` stamps the pointee typedef on the stored `TypedNil`, `elemOf` reads `TypeDef.Elem` for synthesized pointer typedefs) so `psp.M` binds instead of panicking (`text_pass_nilassign`). `nilptr2.go` — address-of through a nil base panics: `&*p` emits `p` + `OpNilPtrCheck` (the & and * cancel but Go still runs the dereference's nil check), `&s.f`/`&a[i]` on a nil `*S`/`*[N]T` raise the nil-pointer panic at OpFieldRef/OpIndexRef creation (`checkAddrBase`), `&s[i]` on a nil slice raises index-out-of-range, and `&(expr)` unwraps parens (`text_pass_addrnil`); `(&a).h()` on a captured `a` emits `OpUpvalRef` instead of trapping (`text_pass_upvalref`). `closure2.go` — function-local `type` decls resolve in sibling decls' embed specs: each typedef snapshots its visible local typedefs (`TypeDef.LocalTypes`, inner shadows outer) and `resolveTypeRef` consults it before the package index (`text_pass_localtypeembed`). `peano.go`/`method5.go` — a named value assigns to an unnamed target with an identical underlying type (`type Number *Number`: `*x` (Number) binds a `*Number` param — `coerceConcrete` accepts `tdShapeEq` when `!tagIsNamed(td)`; peano's remaining `stack exhausted` trap is legitimate since VM frames consume host stack) and nested `Named{T, Named{U, v}}` tags peel through `convert`'s loop so `int(v)` reaches the underlying value (`text_pass_assignnamed`). `convert4.go` — slice→array conversions: `[N]T(s)` copies the first N elements (Go 1.20; nil slice yields the zero array), `(*[N]T)(s)`/`(*[N]T)(nil)` share/keep the pointer semantics (a nil slice converts to nil only for `[0]T`, a longer array conversion panics like Go), an array source converts only to the identical array type, and `&s[i]` refs compare by backing element slots (`IndexRef.Slice` exported) so `&ss[0] == &s5[0]` through `(*[N]T)` views (`text_pass_convarray`). Remaining TRAPs are all boundary-class: `unsafe.Pointer` (22 programs — needs a pointer value model, tracked in #40), GC fidelity (`SetFinalizer`/`MemStats`/`MemProfileRate` stats are bound but no real GC), `reflect.ValueOf` on script values, `unsafe.String`/`Offsetof`, plus gcgort (a genuine Go deadlock) and copy/divmod throughput HANGs. Num-domain hunt (seed 8111): all 133 SILENTs fixed — conversions producing `Named{T, Named{U, v}}` lost the declared tag through binary ops (flattened in `convert`'s KindNamedBasic arm — `num_pass_namedconv`); `unsignedIntTyp` lacked `uint` so wide unsigneds rendered signed, `var u uint = <wide>` stayed an untagged `GoValue{uint64}` (coerceConcrete tags a sized-int slot), and `float64`/`float32` of unsigned sources read int64 bits signed (`convert` records `srcUnsigned` while peeling, `hostFloat` reads boxed host ints/uints by kind — `num_pass_uintdom`); unary `-`/`^` on a tagged wide box dropped the tag to `uint64` (`retag` wraps `GoValue` results too). Seed-8111 guard re-run: 0 SILENT. Corpus sweep (seed 20261003): `recover1.go` now passes byte-identical — `recover()` follows gorecover's one-frame rule (exactly one non-wrapper frame to the panic's unwind; `defer recover()` has zero and never catches), the deferred-builtin push is a zero-count sentinel, a normally-completing frame stays on the stack while its defers drain under an outer panic, and a panic consumed mid-drain hands the remaining defers back to the outer context like `recovery` landing on the frame's deferreturn (`text_value_deferrecover`). `devirtualization_nil_panics.go` fully passes — `runtime.Callers` now splices the unwound frames in at the unwind boundary (deferred chain → unwinding innermost-first → live frames below) instead of appending them at the end, so `CallersFrames` finds the panicking frame at its call line (`text_value_defercallers`). `copy.go` verified throughput-only (correct output, ~50s wall — boundary class). `recover.go` advances to the reflect boundary — a panic raised inside a deferred call during a normal-return drain unwinds into the still-live owner frame, so recover()'s boundary is the deferred call's own slot (`runOneDefer` roots `unwindDepth` at the invoke index, not `dpos`) (`text_value_deferboundary`). Harness: panic-vs-panic divergences where minigo's panic text is one go itself produced elsewhere in the program are reclassified Pass — Go leaves the order of non-call operands unspecified, so racing panic sources have no canonical winner (removes a recurring generator artifact). Corpus sweep (seed 20261004): `reflectmethod7.go` now passes — `Type.Method`/`MethodByName` Func values wrap the method-expression the compiler would emit (`vc.Member(typedef, name)` — a value receiver under `reflect.PointerTo` derefs its `*T` argument, promoted members re-select on the concrete receiver) and carry the engine's caller context, recorded on the reflect Env at every builtin entry (`Env.note`/`caller` — one Env backs one engine's reflect bind), so `Method.Func.Call`/`CallSlice`/`Value.MethodByName` no longer trap `needs a caller context` (`reflect_method-func-call`). Remaining corpus SILENTs are all boundary-class: GC fidelity — the finalizer/MemStats/heap-profile programs (closure, deferfin, finprofiled, gc2, mallocfin, stackobj, stackobj3, tinyfin) count interpreter host allocations a no-GC engine cannot reproduce — the `initialize.go` reflect.DeepEqual/unsafe.Pointer boundary (#40), `linkmain_run.go` harness tmpdir paths, `gcgort.go` a genuine Go deadlock, and throughput HANGs (copy/divmod/init1/heapsampling/winbatch all exit 0 with longer timeouts). Corpus sweep (seed 20261004) round 2 — five more files pass byte-identical, and two earlier "boundary" calls were wrong: `recover.go` — `reflect.MakeFunc` bound (the produced func is a `*runtime.BuiltinFunc` wrapped in an RValue carrying the declared typedef; call args pack into `[]reflect.Value` borrowing the param typedef for `checkCallArgs`, callback results map back 0/1/N through NIL/value/Tuple; `return nil` results mean no outputs) (`reflect_makefunc`); `closure.go`/`gc2.go` — `runtime.ReadMemStats` no longer proxies the host allocator: it serves one stable snapshot, so script delta checks (`n0 != m.Mallocs`) stop false-positiving on interpreter activity (`memstats_stable`); `initialize.go` — the "DeepEqual/unsafe.Pointer boundary" was actually keyed array-literal elements skipping `v.coerce` (raw `UConst` elems vs positional `Named{byte}` — `s.Elems[idx[i]] = v.coerce(...)`) (`keyedlit_coerce`); `gcgort.go` — the "genuine Go deadlock" was a masked `interface {} is complex64, not complex64` trap: `typeMatches` had no `*runtime.GoValue` case so host-boxed values failed every concrete assert (now compares `reflect.TypeOf(gv.V).String()` to `tdName(td)`); the proc-kill that turned the panic into a host deadlock stays an observability gap — a goroutine panic that poisons the proc surfaces only as `all goroutines are asleep` when the root call is blocked inside a host `WaitGroup.Wait` (`govalue_assert`). Remaining corpus items are all boundary-class: the GC-finalizer family (closure-adjacent `deferfin`/`tinyfin`/`mallocfin`/`finprofiled`/`stackobj`/`stackobj3` — `SetFinalizer` is a documented no-op with no GC), `peano.go` frame limit, the `unsafe.Pointer`/`unsafe.String`/`unsafe.Offsetof`/`runtime.FuncForPC` traps (pointer-model and host-PC boundaries), `linkmain_run.go` nondeterministic tmpdir, and the six throughput HANGs.
- [x] difffuzz harness > Harness: a usecasefuzz-style skill for hand-written scenario programs, sharing difffuzz's verdict contract and `testdata/difffuzz` regression format — `.claude/skills/casefuzz` covers realistic `func main()` scenarios a generator cannot write (same PASS/TRAP/SILENT/CRASH/HANG contract, `{main.go,want.stdout[,PENDING]}` pinned dirs, `TestDiffRegressions` enforcement); first pinned scenario: `text_pass_jsonlines` (JSONL decode → filter → sort → re-emit).
- [x] difffuzz harness > **reflect-domain findings** (first `difffuzz gen -domain reflect` run — ~1600 probes, 12 unique bugs after shrink+fold; pinned seeds below):
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > panic-message wording (pinned `reflect_panic-message_{2dc460b5,51bbab42,5566ea1a,e2ee78f2}`): facade panics now carry reflect's method+type wording — `call of reflect.Value.<name> on zero Value` (mustValid takes the method name), `Field of non-struct type X`/`FieldByIndex of non-struct type X`/`In of non-func type X`/`Elem of invalid type X`/`Key of non-map type X`/`Len of non-array type X`/`NumIn/NumOut/In/Out of non-func type X`/`Bits of non-arithmetic Type X`, `FieldByIndex` checks the struct kind at every level, `Set` reports `using unaddressable value` before assignability like Go, and `Type.String()` renders func/interface/struct via `TypGoSpelling` (`func(int) int`, `func(...int)`, `interface { M(int) string }`, `struct { x int "tag" }`). Index-argument leaks also fixed — facade panics now omit the index like Go (`reflect: Method index out of range`, `reflect: string index out of range`, `reflect: slice index out of range`, `reflect: Field index out of range` on Value / `reflect: Field index out of bounds` on Type, plus `reflect: array index out of range` for array storage); interface `Type.Method` returns the zero Method out-of-range and drops the receiver from Method.Type, and `Value.MethodByName` reports the bound signature (`func() int`) instead of `<unnamed>` (pinned: `reflect_panic-index-wording`). Setter wording landed too — the settable check now runs before the kind gate in every `Set*` (`reflect.Value.SetX using unaddressable/unexported`), `expectKind` no longer double-prefixes (`reflect: reflect:`), `Set` reports `reflect.Set:`/`call of reflect.Value.Set on zero Value`, `SetBytes` answers `reflect.Value.SetBytes of non-byte slice`, `SetLen`/`SetCap` report `slice length/capacity out of range`, `SetIterKey`/`SetIterValue` panic `reflect: Value.SetIterKey called before Next` ahead of the target check and assign under `reflect.MapIter.SetKey`/`SetValue`, `SetMapIndex` panics `assignment to entry in nil map` on insert (delete stays a no-op) with `reflect.Value.SetMapIndex:` assignability, `Call`/`CallSlice` gate on Func (`call of reflect.Value.Call on int Value`), `CanInterface`/`Interface`/`Overflow*` panic `on zero Value` and wrong-kind values report `call of reflect.Value.OverflowX on Y Value`, `Index`/`Slice` on a nil slice answer `reflect: slice index out of range`/`reflect.Value.Slice: slice index out of bounds`, `FieldByIndex` uses `Field` wording past the first level, and `byte`/`rune` spell `uint8`/`int32` in panic types (pinned: `reflect_panic-setters`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > `SetBytes` gained the slice-of-uint8 gate after the settable check (`reflect_missing-panic_878aebdc`); `Convert` gained the `ConvertibleTo` legality gate reporting `reflect.Value.Convert: value of type X cannot be converted to type Y` (`reflect_missing-panic_aed2e610`, `reflect_panic-message_9dce66f7`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > `Index`/`Len`/`Cap` on a named Value never terminated: the Named-unwrap recursion forwarded `v.ref` into `wrap`, and `get()` dereferences the same location back into the `*runtime.Named` — `unwrap()` now drops the ref (keeps `v.td` for kind strings; named-array elements lose ref addressability as a corner case). The `Index` panic now matches Go's `call of reflect.Value.Index on int Value` (was `<unrecovered: >` standalone, stack-overflow in-process).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > `Convert` into the identical type panicked spuriously — `typeOfValue` returned bare name typedefs `{Name:"int"}` (Kind unset → `KindStruct` iota 0), so `New(int)`'s zero-of produced `*runtime.Struct` values that `Int()` rejects; `typeOfValue` now returns `runtime.BasicTypedef` objects for builtins (`reflect_spurious-panic_d9b9abe8`); `TypeOf(func(...int) int).In(0)` resolves the `*ast.Ellipsis` param to the `[]T` slice typedef (`reflect_spurious-panic_cafe4eb9`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > `Value.Slice` accepts strings — Go slices string Values since 1.17, with `reflect.Value.Slice: string slice index out of bounds` / `slice index out of bounds` / `slice of unaddressable array` bounds wording (`reflect_value_55b51b96`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > `reflect.Value.SetCap` unbound — `runtime trap: no member SetCap` (facade lacks the Go 1.20 method; TRAP, no seed). Fixed — facade `RValue.SetCap` reslices the header in place with `s.Elems[:len:n]` (len preserved, named tag kept), unexported → `using value obtained using unexported field`, unaddressable → `using unaddressable value`, non-slice → `call of reflect.Value.SetCap on X Value`, out-of-range → `slice capacity out of range in SetCap` (pinned: `reflect_set-cap`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > builtin `error` typedef carried `MReqs` but no interface AST — `ifaceSigsOf` resolved zero signatures, so `Implements(error)`/`TypeAssert[error]` degenerated to `any` (`TypeAssert[error](ValueOf(42))` → `(42, true)`; `Type.Method` on `error` returned the zero Method). Fixed — the typedef now holds `interface{ Error() string }` as `Anon`, so Implements checks name AND signature (`*PtrErr` implements, `PtrErr` doesn't, `Error() int` is rejected) and `Type.Method(0)` reports `Error func() string` (pinned: `reflect_error-implements`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > `fmt` on an interface-typed value ignores the declared interface type — `var e error = v; fmt.Println(e)` dispatches `String()` on the concrete payload where Go prefers `Error()` for `error`-typed values; a nil interface's `%T` spells `runtime.Nil` where Go writes `<nil>` (pre-existing fmt divergence surfaced by `reflect.TypeAssert` probes). Fixed — `scriptTypeString` spells `runtime.Nil` as `<nil>` (pinned: `reflect_nil-iface-t`) and the fmt method dispatch consults `Error()` before `String()` on `%v %s %q %x` like Go's `handleMethods` (pinned: `reflect_error-string-order`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > context-dependent divergence ×2 — `TypeOf(r_struct).Field(0).Type` panicking `call of reflect.Type.Field on type []byte` and `ValueOf(r_sptr).Elem().Type().Kind()` diverging appeared only in multi-probe programs. Root cause: `RValue.Set` had no assignability gate, so `ValueOf(&r_struct).Elem().Set(ValueOf(r_bytes))` silently overwrote the shared global and later probes read it as `[]byte`. Fixed by the `reflect.Set:` assignability check in the setter-wording sweep — both corrupting `Set`s now panic `value of type X is not assignable to type Y` like Go (pinned two-probe seed: `reflect_shared-global-set`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > package qualifiers spelled import paths instead of package names — `reflect.Type.String()` on `*T`/`[]T` composites rendered `*<dir>/tmp/x.RTag` / `difffuzz.example/shrink_89.RTag`, `Type.PkgPath()` returned the synthesized dir path (`<dir>/tmp/x` where Go reports `main`) or the path itself for unnamed types (Go: `""`), and `hash of unhashable type` panics doubled the qualifier. Fixed — `TypGoSpelling` requalifies `pkg.Path`-prefixed idents/selectors by the declaring package's clause name (imported selectors spell `http.Client`, not `net/http.Client`), `typeName`/`msgTypeName` prefer `td.File`'s package clause, `PkgPath` reports `""` for unnamed types and `main` for package-main types, and composite typedefs (`pointerTd`/`compositeTd`/`Addr`) now carry `Pkg`/`File` so display spellings can resolve the declared name (pinned: `reflect_pkg-qualify`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > host `int64`/`uint64` results surfaced as bare `int` — `Int()`/`Uint()` returns, `int64(x)` conversions, and host int results (`time.Second.Nanoseconds()`) all `%T`-spelled `int` where Go spells the sized name (`reflect.Int64()` receiver type assertions also trapped `cannot use int64 as int`). Fixed — `goValueOf` now wraps concrete int64/uint64 in a sized `Named` tag (`Tag(sizedIntTyp(kind), v)`) while interface-unwrapped payloads stay bare (script `int` share int64 storage; only `rv.Kind()` distinguishes) (pinned: `reflect_int64-tag`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > `%#v` dropped the GoString type wrapper and `%T` spelled funcs as `func` — Go wraps pointer-ish values `(T)(0xADDR)` (`(chan int)(0x…)`, `(*int)(0x…)`) and spells func signatures in full (`func(int, int) int`). Fixed — `renderValue`'s `#`-flag arms emit `(T)(0x…)` for chans/funcs/scalar-ptr Cells, `%T`/`scriptTypeString` spell funcs via `funcSigSpelling` (AST decl sig → `TypGoSpelling`; builtin `Target` falls back to the host's own `%T`; named func types still spell `main.F`), and `fmt.Print`/`Println` carry `Target` so `%T fmt.Println` spells `func(...interface {}) (int, error)` (pinned: `reflect_gostring-ptrs`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > bound `strings.Reader` was unresolvable — `reflect.TypeOf((*strings.Reader)(nil)).Elem()` spelled `<nil>` and `strings.Reader{}` trapped `undefined: strings.Reader` (the `strings` bind table carried Builder but not Reader). Fixed — `strings.Reader` is bound via `hostType` like Builder, so the `*T` Elem typedef resolves to the named host type and the composite literal constructs it (pinned: `reflect_bound-star-elem`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > `reflect.TypeOf(host composite literal)` read `*T` — `reflect.TypeOf(bytes.Buffer{}).String()` spelled `*bytes.Buffer` where Go spells `bytes.Buffer` (host composites box into a pointer cell, and `var b T` zeros lost the declared tag too). Fixed — `HostNew` literals and `runtime.Zero` tag the box `Named{td, GoValue{*T}}` so TypeOf/%T read `T` while the pointer-shaped payload keeps pointer methods callable; `namedMember`/`fmtArg`/`asRValue`/`asRType`/`methodSetOfValue`/`valueOfValue` unwrap the tag on the host path, `typeMethodsU` reports the boxed host type's reflect method set, and `exprOf`/`TypGoSpelling`/`typedefSpelling` stop double-qualifying bound-package names (`*bytes.bytes.Buffer`) (pinned: `reflect_host-composite`).
- [x] difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > `%T` on facade-bound methods read `func()` — `fmt.Printf("%T", reflect.ValueOf(x).Len)` spelled `func()` where Go spells `func() int` (facade method Functions carry no Decl). Fixed — `hostMember` records the bound method value as `bf.Target`, so `funcSigSpelling` spells the receiver-less signature (`Len` → `func() int`, `Interface` → `func() interface {}`). `RType.Method`'s return type still leaks `*minireflect.Method` (facade implementation type — structurally unfixable spelling).

- difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > reflect-domain findings round 2 > `Call`/`CallSlice` run their signature gates before the caller-context check — `callSig` prefers the typedef's reflect signature (a Method Func's receiver counts as an input), `Call` reports arity on vc-less Func Values, and `CallSlice` gates variadic → exact `len(in) == NumIn` arity → last-arg `[]Elem` assignability (`using X as type []Y`) like Go (pinned: `reflect_call-vc-order`, `reflect_callslice-gates`).
- difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > reflect-domain findings round 2 > `Type.In`/`Out`/composite-element resolution keeps `*T` — a new `resolveExpr` resolves full type exprs (StarExpr → KindPointer td) instead of the embed-spec resolver's base peel, so `In(0)` of `func(*RStr)` spells `*main.RStr` and `[]*T`/`map[K]*T` elems retain the star (pinned: `reflect_in-star-param`).
- difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > reflect-domain findings round 2 > `Value.Grow` bound (Go 1.20) — addressability gates before the slice kind (`reflect: reflect.Value.Grow using unaddressable value` / `call of ... on int Value`), capacity models Go's growslice steps (empty allocates need, <256 doubles, quarter-steps past) (pinned: `reflect_grow`).
- difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > reflect-domain findings round 2 > difffuzz generator: methoded `RStr` + `RNSlice`/`RNStr`/`RNMap` prelude types, 78 kind-tagged seeds incl. `SliceOf`/`MapOf`/`PtrTo`/`ChanOf`/`ArrayOf` constructors and `MakeSlice`/`MakeMap`/`New`, plus `rFieldType`/`rAppendV`/`rAppendSliceV`/`rCopyI` helpers (PR #242).
- difffuzz harness > reflect-domain findings > reflect-domain findings round 2 > difffuzz harness: `Runner.mask` collapses pointer hex addresses `0x[0-9a-fA-F]{6,}` → `0x…` before user masks, so `%p`/`%#v` address decimals stop flipping PASS↔SILENT verdicts (PR #220).
- [x] difffuzz harness > `io.Writer` produced by a script-side implementation is not usable as a `fmt.Fprintf` target yet (`asWriter` only unwraps host boxes). → asWriterVM adapts a script Write method for the fmt.Fprint* family; io.Copy/bufio/csv writer slots still reject script values.
- [x] difffuzz harness > Refactor sweep of the sweep-report proposals ([docs/sketch/ja/difffuzz-todo-sweep-report.md](./docs/sketch/ja/difffuzz-todo-sweep-report.md) round-7; Stack #139, #128–#138): `runtime.Map` Insert/Delete/Clear API, fscope `binding` record + `resolveName` ladder, `runtime.TypIdentical`/`TypIdenticalStrict` type identity, `runtime/errors.go` panic constructors per Go error family, `c.fresh` synthetic-name mint, `runtime.Tag`/`TagOf`/`Unwrap` for `Named` tags. Eql gates fixed along the way: cross-type typed nils/slices/maps compare false (was true/panic), nil-map-assign and close-nil-chan payloads are now `error`-typed `PlainError`, `Recover()` boxes any error payload.

- [x] **`TestHostParkLeak` was timing-flaky on CI** (`concurrency_test.go`): fixed — the assertion was a net `runtime.NumGoroutine` delta, so any unrelated goroutine death inside the poll window masked the leaked +1 permanently (reproduced locally by parking a transient goroutine that dies mid-run); the rewritten test drops counting and has the spawned goroutine prove its own survival — a test-bound `parkprobe` builtin signals that the goroutine reached the park point, after which the script parks on a real `sync.WaitGroup` as before (`testdata/hostpark`).

- [x] **Windows verification round** (`make test` on Windows Server 2022 + all four fuzz harnesses re-run; [docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-windows.md](./docs/sketch/ja/fuzz-windows.md)): `make test` green; usecasefuzz/langfuzz/concfuzz/convfuzz all reproduce Linux verdicts. Windows fixes — `find`/CRLF/`filepath.ToSlash` for display paths, `os.SameFile` for exec-dir matching, `cmd /c` for shell builtins in testdata, `.gitattributes` (`eol=lf`). Interpreter bugs found along the way (platform-independent): conversion-produced sized ints lost their type tag (`-uint8(5)` → -5) now `Named`-wrapped, constant expressions fold via `go/constant` (`1<<100>>50` = 2^50; unrepresentable consts and `x/0` trap at compile), `%T` prints `int32`/`uint8` for `rune`/`byte` aliases.
- [x] **Interfaces**: method sets, dynamic dispatch, `any`/`interface{}` values (types and asserts). Satisfaction is duck-typed over the runtime method set (embedded-interface members, promoted methods via `FindMethod`); constraint elements (`~T`, unions) are approximated as satisfied.
- [x] **Generics**: monomorphize-on-use — `F[T]`/`S[T]{...}`/method calls bind type parameters at instantiation sites (`OpInstantiate` + compile-time `binds` overlay). Call-site inference binds undeclared type args from argument values (`Id(40)` infers `T=int`), `var z T` yields T's zero through `OpCoerce`, and `~T`/union/`comparable` constraints are checked at instantiation (a wrong arg traps, like Go's rejection). Remaining gaps: inference is argument-only (no result/context-driven inference, no constraint-type inference), and a generic typedef's fields don't re-resolve `T` on read (they get the bound typedef's zero/value instead).
- [x] **Type assertions / type switches**: `x.(T)` (panic + comma-ok via `OpAssert`/`OpAssertOK`) and `switch v := x.(type)` with per-case narrowing; `case nil` works.
- [x] **Special forms (`SPECIAL_CALL`)**: `OpSpecialCall` resolves `pkg.Sym` to a canonical `runtime.SymbolID` through the file's import scope and dispatches to a `SpecialFunc` registered on the engine/bound package — before the member is materialized. `QuotedCall` carries the call AST + caller local/upval maps; `SpecialContext` (`Position`/`File`/`Package`/`Eval`/`Call`/`Format`/`Errorf`) compiles args lazily in the caller's scope (`compile.ExprScoped`), so partial-argument evaluation and unevaluated quoting both work.
- [x] **Full init-order analysis**: the topo sort now follows transitive deps through function bodies (`var x = f()` waits on every package-level name `f` transitively reads).
- [x] **`&s.f`, `x[i]++`, compound assign on non-idents**: `FieldRef`/`IndexRef` cell-views unify `runtime.Deref`/`SetRef` with `Cell`; `x[i]++`, `s.f++`, `(*p)++`, `s.f += v`, `m[k] *= v`, `*p = v` all work.
- [x] **Spread calls**: `f(args...)` argument spreading at call sites (`OpCall` B-flag).
- [x] **`fallthrough`**: switch clause fall-through, including into `default`; falling out of the final clause traps.
- [x] **Labels / `goto`**: labeled `for`/`range`/`switch`/`select`/`switch x.(type)`, `break L`/`continue L`, forward and backward `goto` (unresolved targets trap at run time). Note: `goto` into nested blocks is not rejected (Go's scoping rule is unchecked).
- [x] **`LazyInit` mode**: `WithInitMode(LazyInit)` lets `Member` answer function/type queries without running initializers; var/const still require Ready.
- [x] **`AllowedRoots` option**: `WithAllowedRoots(roots...)` restricts located directories to the given roots (checked in `Locate`/`LocateDir`).
- [x] **Stdlib acceleration**: intrinsic bindings for fmt/errors/strings/strconv/sort/slices/maps/os/time are installed into every engine via `Bind`; host values are boxed as `*runtime.GoValue` with reflective method dispatch.
- [x] **`OP_EVAL_AST` migration bridge**: `OpEvalAST` keeps an AST fragment as a chunk constant and compiles it via `compile.Expr` on first execution; `Engine.EvalExpr` is the public entry point.
- [x] **Conformance harness**: `conformance_test.go` runs the shared-subset corpus under both engines and diffs normalized results (known v1 divergences documented).
- [x] **Real concurrency semantics**: `go` spawns real host goroutines sharing a per-run *process* — each spawned VM is confined to its goroutine while packages, channels and the materialization cache are shared. Channels are genuine `chan Value` (blocking send/recv, honored buffer capacity, close waking parked peers); `select` is a real `reflect.Select` (operand-eval order, random ready choice, blocking without default, empty select parks). `VMCaller.Spawn`/`runtime.Task` expose the spawn machinery to hosts (task-run's parallel `Deps`). Process exit is modeled on `proc.done`: the root `Call` returning — or a goroutine's panic failing the process — closes it and unwinds parked ops with a `recover()`-invisible `procExit`, so `PanicInGoroutine` fails the run like Go's crash. Lazy package init runs on the *triggering* VM (`MemberV` runner), `TypeDef.HostNew` backs `sync.WaitGroup`/`Mutex`/`RWMutex`/`Once` as real host types, and `sync`/`time.After`/`time.NewTimer`/`NewTicker`/`runtime.NumGoroutine`/`Gosched` intrinsics are bound. Verified under `testing/synctest` bubbles (blocking, fake-clock `Sleep`/`After`, detached-goroutine cleanup) and `-race`. Approximations kept: root deadlocks hang like Go's crash rather than trapping (no goroutine preemption), and script cells/maps share storage without script-visible races documented as Go-like UB.
- [x] **Package objects / symbol introspection** ([docs/sketch/plan-package-introspection.md](./docs/sketch/plan-package-introspection.md)): the `minigo.dev/inspect` stub package exposes the 3-layer API — index (`PackageOf`/`DirOf`/`FileOf` locators, `Decls`/`Symbol`/`Files`/`Imports`/`UsedSymbols`), syntax (`Fields`/`Methods`/`Signature`/`TypeParams`/`Def`/`Doc`/`Pos`, `TypeExpr` navigation `Children`/`UnRef`/`UnWrap`/`Origin`/`SymbolID`/`Resolve`/`SameType`), and value (`Value`/`TypeOf`); `OwnerOf`/`PathOf`/`SymbolIDOf`/`SymbolOf` map symbols and values back to package and decl (struct instances included; bare type names are a minigo extension, not valid Go). Bound packages enumerate as Kind:`host` pseudo-decls whose `Signature` is recovered from `BuiltinFunc.Target` (the real Go func value, stashed by `h.fn*`); `Package.Standard` splits stdlib from module packages. The REPL gained `:cd <ref>`/`:cd -` (pseudo dot-import with `ImportRef.AllNames` — unexported names resolve) and `:ls [ref]`.
- [x] **inspect: interface type members** — interface decls now read through `Fields` (member elements: named method specs carry `Names` + a FuncType `TypeExpr`, embedded/constraint elements come back `Embedded`) and the two split views from the plan's open question: `MReqs` (method specs only) and `IEmbeds` (embedded/constraint elements as TypeExprs — `~int|~int64` returns one BinaryExpr whose Children are the tilde terms). `SymbolID`/`Resolve` chase embedded names back to their decls.
- [x] **inspect: `SourceOf`** — locator bypassing `Bind` shadows: `SourceOf("strings")` locates and indexes the GOROOT source into a private engine cache (`srcs`, never `pkgs`/`byDir`), so `Symbol`/`Signature`/`Fields` read real decls (param names included) while the bound package keeps answering `PackageOf` and real imports. Unbound paths return the canonical package — `SourceOf == PackageOf` there. Value-layer access on real GOROOT source can still trap on unimplemented constructs; index/syntax layers are the intended use (documented on the stub).
- [x] **REPL `:cd` write-mode** — `:pin`/`:unpin` inside `:cd` publishes declarations into the entered package's `Globals` (monkey-patching; visible to every importer in the session engine). Cells of package vars are aliased into the repl scope at `Pin` (after `EnsureReady`) so `x = v` writes through OpSetGlobal's shared cell; `x := v` reuses or shadows into a published cell (also how bound non-cell members get patched); func/type decls bind their materialized value into both scopes (one identity — reload eviction skips them — with `Pkg` retargeted to the entered package so patches survive `:cd -`); method decls graft onto the entered index's `Types[recv].Methods` (or onto a live published typedef for repl-declared receivers) and evict the cached typedef so the next access rebuilds the method set; the decl's repl-file import table is registered into the package (`graftScope`) so patched bodies resolve repl imports. `:unpin`/`:cd -`/`:cd` elsewhere decouple: written names stay in the package, borrowed repl names drop, and bindings shadowed by an alias are restored (the saved binding object itself, not a snapshot). `:ls` marks published decls `patch`; `:cd` shows `[pin]`. Caveats kept from the plan: package objects are shared, so patches are session-wide; a package var keeps its declared type (`Typ` coercion on the shared cell), and consts stay read-only.
- [x] **Go 1.26/1.27 language deltas**: `new(expr)` initializers (1.26 — `new(42)` gives a `*int` to a copy of 42; the builtin distinguishes a `*TypeDef` arg from a value); self-referential type constraints (1.26 — `type Adder[A Adder[A]]` instantiates; `checkTArgs` resolves named/`T[...]` constraints through `ResolveType` and verifies interface method requirements via `TypeMethods`); generic methods (1.27 — `func (l List[E]) Map[R any](...)` binds through `Function.TParams`/`TConstraints`, instantiated by `OpInstantiate` on `BoundMethod` or inferred from call args — receiver-prepended `args[0]` is skipped — and stays out of the interface method set, like Go); composite-literal promoted-field keys (1.27 — `Wrap{V: 9}` initializes embedded `E.V` via a breadth-first `promotedField`; shallowest wins, ambiguity and pointer-embed keys trap); generalized function-type inference (1.27 — `var f func(int) int = Id`, composite elements, `T(x)` conversions, channel sends and call args infer `T` through `unifyType` signature unification). Testdata: `minigo/testdata/go1267` (values verified against go1.27.1). Found implementing: `selectMember` never consulted `Typ.Methods` on Slice/Map/Chan — named container methods were unreachable, fixed by `typedMember`; `resolveTypeRef` peeled `T[...]` to the base typedef, so a declared `List[int]` field type never matched a specialized `List[int]` literal — `instantiateRef` now mirrors `specializeType` for AST-level resolution. Remaining gaps: two specialization paths exist (`specializeType` on values, `instantiateRef` on AST) and should converge; `unifyType` is one-directional and does not flag conflicting binds; generic method expressions must be *called* (`List[int].Reduce(l, ...)`) — binding `List[int].Reduce` uninstantiated is not rejected like the Go compiler does; `new(expr)` evaluates eagerly, so `new(ch <- x)`/side-effecting args run before allocation; promoted-field literal keys do not yet recurse selector chains (`Outer{Inner.F: v}` stays a trap, matching Go's rejection of that spelling).

_The `[-]` item below is **still open** — its compact live entry is in `TODO.md`; original status text kept verbatim for provenance:_

- [ ] **Intrinsic coverage + element types**: fmt/errors/strings/strconv/sort/slices/maps/os/time are intrinsics, plus bytes, unicode, unicode/utf8, math, regexp, encoding/base64, encoding/hex, encoding/json (script-shaped `Unmarshal` returns the decoded tree), net/url, html, path — host objects (`*bytes.Buffer`, `*regexp.Regexp`, `*base64.Encoding`) box as `*runtime.GoValue` with reflective method dispatch; plus `unsafe` (`Sizeof`/`Alignof` as 64-bit boxed approximations; `Offsetof` errors), `runtime` (`GOOS`/`GOARCH`/`Version`/`NumCPU`/`GOMAXPROCS` pass through, `NumGoroutine`→1, `GC` no-op), `path/filepath` (path math + `Glob`/`WalkDir`/`EvalSymlinks`/`Abs`/`Rel` anchored at the engine cwd), and `os/exec` (`Command`/`LookPath`, unrestricted engines only). `os` gained the full file-I/O surface (`Stat`/`Lstat`/`ReadFile`/`WriteFile`/`Mkdir`/`MkdirAll`/`Remove`/`RemoveAll`/`Rename`/`Truncate`/`ReadDir`/`Open`/`Create`/`OpenFile`/`MkdirTemp`/`CreateTemp`, `IsNotExist`-family + sentinels, `O_*`/`Mode*`/seek consts) with each path checked against `AllowedRoots` per call — file APIs stay usable in restricted mode but confined. Ordering helpers still only sort int64/float64/string elements natively (SortFunc takes a script predicate); `errors.As` is approximated (binds the first non-nil cause — the target type cannot be spelled on the script side). Measured gap (round 16): a member missing from a bound package still traps `undefined` — the bind map shadows the source decl, there is no per-member fall-through; unbound stdlib packages DO attempt GOROOT interpretation but real source trips immediately — `uintptr(0)` conversions are not a builtin (`utf8`), untyped hex consts above MaxInt64 trap (`math/bits.go`), and `internal/bytealg` members are assembly stubs (`strings` internals). `strings.Builder` materializes its TypeDef but the method set stays empty — `bytes.NewBuffer`/`NewBufferString` is the bound stand-in. Extending coverage = more `Bind` entries, not source interpretation.

- [x] **Restricted-mode host-surface policy**: `WithHostPolicy(func(path, sym string) bool)` filters every `Bind` symbol (stdlib intrinsics included). Per-call path checks landed: `resolve.BuildConfig.CheckPath` resolves symlinks through the nearest existing ancestor, and every filesystem intrinsic routes through `Engine.fsPath` (virtual-cwd anchor + roots check), so file I/O is bound but confined. `os/exec` and the env/argv/stdio surface bind only when unrestricted (a spawned process escapes path confinement); `os.Exit` always traps; `WithOutput(w)` routes fmt/print output. Network I/O has no intrinsics yet — when it lands it follows the same per-call shape.
- [x] **Virtual cwd + `os.Chdir`/`os.Getwd`**: `WithWorkingDir(dir)`/`Engine.WorkingDir()` give each engine its own cwd (defaults to `NewEngine`'s startDir, inherited by sessions); script `os.Chdir` moves it without touching the host process — `host.Getwd` still reports the real one. `filepath.Abs`/`Rel`, relative fs paths, and `exec.Cmd.Dir` all anchor there.
- [x] **Host-struct fields on `*runtime.GoValue`**: `selectMember`/`setField` probe exported struct fields through pointer chains (get and set), falling back to the reflective method set; `toReflectValue` marshals writes/method args element-wise for slices/maps with assignable-or-convertible scalars, so `cmd.Dir = "sub"`, `cmd.Stdout = os.Stdout`, `f.Write("text")` work on `*exec.Cmd`/`*os.File`.
- [x] **Task-runner example**: `examples/task-run` — a mage/go-task-style runner on minigo ([docs/sketch/plan-task-runner.md](./docs/sketch/plan-task-runner.md)). Taskfiles are Go files (`//go:build task`); exported `func()`/`func() error`/`func(...string)` decls are tasks; the bound `task` package provides `Deps`/`SerialDeps`/`F`/`Sh`/`Run`/`RunIn`/`Output`/`Target`/`Log`/`Env`. `Deps` runs deps in parallel on spawned goroutines (a shared `depStates` map gives both modes dedup and ancestry-based cycle detection; a dep error fails the run); `SerialDeps` keeps serial ordering. Follow-ups are in the plan file (task arg typing, discovery, namespaces).
- [x] **`[]byte` conversion in script**: `convert` is now a VM method covering the special `string`↔`[]byte`/`[]rune` forms plus general `T(x)` conversions on slice/map/chan/pointer/struct typedefs when underlying shapes match — compared through a binds-aware spelling of the emitted type expression (`byte`→`uint8`, `rune`→`int32` normalized, so `[]byte`↔`[]uint8` both ways work). `[]T` under generics resolves through instantiation `Binds` (`StrToSliceT[byte](s)` works, `StrToSliceT[int]` traps), named chains keep their declared tag (`type C B`, `type A = []byte`, `B(x)` re-wraps in `Named{Typ}`), and host `GoValue`s carrying string/`[]byte`/`[]rune` unbox before converting. Illegal forms trap like Go: `Ints([]int)` (element `MyInt`≠`int`), `[]rune`→`[]byte`, `string`→`[]int`, `map[string]int`→`map[int]int`, mismatched struct fields, `*byte`→`[]byte`, and typed-nil retags across different shapes.
