- [x] **a nil slice/map of a script element type reaches host `any` as untyped nil**: `deepHost` now crosses it as the typed zero (`hostNilOf`: the host shape when it has one, else `[]any(nil)` / `map[any]any(nil)`), and a FuncMap func declaring `[]Pt` gets its nil back — `{{len .Users}}` is 0 and `{{.Users}}` prints `[]`.
- [x] **FuncMap call edge cases**: a script panic reaches the template as its payload (`error calling p: boom`; `funcMapPanicValue`; only FuncMap funcs, other callbacks keep the `*runtime.Panic`), also from a template run on another goroutine (`VM.CallCatching`); a non-func FuncMap value crosses as an ordinary `any`, so `Template.Funcs` panics recoverably with `value for bad not a function`; `%T` of a script named result stays `int` (inherent, see README "Currently unsupported").
- [x] **`type Tpl template.Template; Tpl(*t)` traps `cannot convert *template.Template`**: a `*p` read tags its value with the pointee's interned typedef (`hostStructTyp`), but the NamedBasic peel recursion dropped that tag before `convertStruct` saw it — and `Tpl` then inherited the host `Name()` method so `t2.Name` printed the method value (gc: declared types keep only fields + promoted members, so `t2.Name` is `x`). fixed: `convertFrom` passes the peeled source tag into its inner calls, `hostStructBox` matches that tag to the target by `HostIdent`, and declared-over-host typedefs resolve only fields + promoted methods, comparing embed depths so a shallower promoted method still wins over a deeper field. difffuzz `hostderef_typedef_conv`. Residual cleared in the same rework: `Tpl(d)` where `d := *t` (inferred var) converts too — the stored deref keeps enough of the pointee typedef for `hostStructBox`'s HostIdent match.
- [x] **Generic pointer method expression traps `cannot use &G as *G[T]`**: `type G[T any] struct{…}; func (g *G[T]) Push(xs ...T)`, then `pe := (*G[string]).Push; pe(g, "a", "b")` traps in `G.Push` instead of appending — the method expression's receiver check compares against the uninstantiated `*G[T]`. Not spread-related. Found in the #1012 review.
- [x] **`--src errors` traps `cannot use interface value as EmptyInterface` in `abi.TypeOf`**: `errors.As`/`errors.Is` from source reach `internal/abi.TypeOf`, whose `(*EmptyInterface)(unsafe.Pointer(&a))` reinterpretation of an `any` is unsupported. Found in the #1012 review.
- `--src errors` traps `cannot use interface value as EmptyInterface` in `abi.TypeOf` > fixed: `internal/reflectlite` is now bound over the minireflect facades (bound internals win over source for stdlib deps, like bytealg), so the eface reinterpretation never runs. Two typed-nil gaps it exposed: `ScriptVal` no longer wraps a returned `*runtime.IfaceNil` in GoValue, and convertFrom keeps an `IfaceNil` whose tag satisfies the interface slot instead of trapping. difffuzz `src_errors_isas`.
- [x] **typed constants are not type-checked against host parameters**: `const c int8 = 5; strconv.Itoa(c)` prints 5 and `strconv.FormatInt(time.Second, 10)` prints 1000000000 where gc rejects both (`cannot use c (constant 5 of type int8) as int value`). A typed constant crossing a bound call should fail when its type is not assignable to the parameter. Found reviewing #1014.
- typed constants are not type-checked against host parameters > fixed: `matBuiltinArgs` checks a `*runtime.Named` arg's typedef and a host-typed arg's concrete type against basic host parameters — `cannot use constant 5 of type int8 as int value` traps like gc's compile error. Bare scalars stay unchecked (a stored int64 cannot distinguish `int` from `int64`), and non-basic parameters fall to the twin lookup; `var i int` passed to an `int64` parameter is a known gap.
- [x] **methods of `reflect.Value` invoked on a Value-of-Value dispatch on the facade, not the wrapped value**: `reflect.ValueOf(v).MethodByName("Type").Call(nil)[0]` prints `*minireflect.RValue` (gc: `int`) — the bound-method receiver unwraps to the `*RValue` facade while gc's receiver resolves to the inner value (gc also reports `Kind()` `struct` directly but `int` through a bound-method call — the receiver layer differs). `IsValid`/`CanInterface`/`CanSet` were fixed by the facade APIType receiver check (PR #1344); the semantic layer the method operates on still differs. Found fixing the #1344 review finding.
- methods of `reflect.Value` invoked on a Value-of-Value dispatch on the facade, not the wrapped value > fixed: `Method`/`MethodByName` bind the facade's methods through `methodRecv` (PR #1344) — `Type`/`Int`/`IsValid` on `reflect.ValueOf(reflect.ValueOf(42))` print `int`/`42`/`true` like gc.
- [x] **`strconv.FormatFloat(1, 'f', -1, 64)` prints 0**: the intrinsic (intrinsics.go `FormatFloat`) asserts `a[0].(float64)`, so an untyped integer constant (materialized as int64) reads as 0, and the `bitSize` argument is ignored (hardcoded 64). Found reviewing #1014.
- `strconv.FormatFloat(1, 'f', -1, 64)` prints 0 > fixed: `strconv.FormatFloat` is bound via `WrapBind` to host `strconv.FormatFloat` — untyped constants coerce to float64 and `bitSize` is honored (`1e39` at 32 bits prints `+Inf` like gc).
- [x] **math/big `Float` from source prints 0**: `new(big.Float).SetFloat64(1).String()` and `big.NewFloat(1e300).Text('g', 5)` print `0` (go: `1`, `1e+300`). Found reviewing #1014.
- [x] **missing stdlib bindings: `sort.SearchInts`, `time.UnixMilli`, `unicode.Han`**: each traps `undefined: ...` (go: `1`, `1970`, `true` for `sort.SearchInts([]int{1, 3, 5}, 3)`, `time.UnixMilli(0).UTC().Year()`, `unicode.Is(unicode.Han, 0x6f22)`). Found reviewing #1014.
- missing stdlib bindings: `sort.SearchInts`, `time.UnixMilli`, `unicode.Han` > fixed: the typed search/sorted wrappers (`SearchInts`/`SearchFloat64s`/`SearchStrings`, `IntsAreSorted`/`Float64sAreSorted`/`StringsAreSorted`) bind via `WrapBind` in internal/stdlib/sort, and `UnixMilli`/`UnixMicro` join `time.Unix` (no `UnixNano` — Go never shipped it). `unicode.Han` was already bound in gen.go. difffuzz `bind_sort_unixmilli`.
- [x] **a spread after extra variadic args runs instead of failing to compile**: `fmt.Printf("%d %v\n", 1, []any{2}...)` prints `1 2` where gc rejects `too many arguments in call to fmt.Printf` (a `xs...` spread must be the only argument for the variadic parameter). Found while rebasing #1014 onto #1012.
- a spread after extra variadic args runs instead of failing to compile > fixed: `spreadArityErr` rejects extras in front of `xs...` where the callee signature is known (script frames, bound funcs with a Target — `fmt.Printf` now carries one), and non-variadic callees trap `cannot use ... in call to non-variadic X`. The `sum(5, xs...)` extension pin (testdata/features SpreadTail) is gone; difffuzz `spread_tail_extra` pins the trap.
- [x] **`reflect` on a bound host scalar constant misses its host methods**: `reflect.TypeOf(time.Monday).NumMethod()` is 0 (go 1; `MethodByName("String")` does work). Found reviewing #1011 (time.Weekday binding).
- `reflect` on a bound host scalar constant misses its host methods > fixed: `RType.NumMethod`/`Method`/`MethodByName` fall back to `hostRTOf(td)` when the typedef backs a bound host scalar (`methodRT`), so `time.Weekday`/`time.Month`/`time.Duration` report and dispatch their host method set. difffuzz `reflect_hostscalar_methods`.
- [x] **`expvar.Map.Set` traps on a zero `expvar.Map`**: `var m expvar.Map; m.Set("a", new(expvar.Int))` trapped `slices.BinarySearch: first arg must be a slice` on the map's nil `keys` field (go prints `{"a": 0}`). The `slices.BinarySearch` intrinsic rejected a nil slice; fixed with the nil-slice entry below. Found reviewing #1015.
- [x] **`syscall` errno and signal members are unbound**: `syscall.EINVAL`, the `syscall.Errno` type and `syscall.SIGUSR1` trap `undefined:` (go prints `invalid argument`, `no such file or directory` for `Errno(2)`, `user defined signal 1`), while `syscall.ENOENT` is bound. Found reviewing #1015.
- `syscall` errno and signal members are unbound > fixed: `Errno` binds as a host-scalar typedef (like `time.Weekday`) so `syscall.Errno(2)` boxes the real host errno — prints via `Error()` and `errors.Is` compares it equal to a bound `GoValue` errno (`HostErrOf` reconstructs a `Named` host scalar into its native error). `SIGUSR1`/`SIGUSR2`/`SIGPIPE` and the common errnos (`EINVAL`, `EPERM`, `EBADF`, …) join the table. difffuzz `syscall_signals_errnos` extended.
- [x] **`json.Marshal` of a `float32` and of a `[]byte` diverge**: `json.Marshal(float32(0.1))` gives `0.10000000149011612` (go `0.1`, encoded at 32-bit precision) and `json.Marshal([]byte("hi"))` gives `[104,105]` (go base64 `"aGk="`). Found scoping the host `any` param shape fix.
- `json.Marshal` of a `float32` and of a `[]byte` diverge > fixed: `goJSONSeen` re-tags a float64 payload as `float32` through the underlying name, and emits a host `[]byte` for byte-element slices — named element types count (`[]MyByte`) while an `[N]byte` array stays element-wise; pinned by `difffuzz/json_marshal_f32_bytes`.
- [x] **converting a string to `encoding/json.RawMessage` traps**: `json.RawMessage("{\"k\":1}")` traps `cannot convert string to encoding/json.RawMessage` (go produces a RawMessage that marshals as a raw literal `{"k":1}`); string→named-`[]byte` host conversion is missing. Found pinning `json_marshal_f32_bytes`; pinned by `difffuzz/json_rawmessage_string`.
- converting a string to `encoding/json.RawMessage` traps > fixed: `HostSliceConv` dereferences a pointer-boxed `HostNew` type (`&RawMessage{}`) so the pointee's slice kind drives the conversion — string and `[]byte`/`*runtime.Slice` sources now mint a value `json.RawMessage` box.
- [x] **`html/template` traps at init**: `html/template` is now host-bound like text/template (docs/sketch/ja/experiment-storybook.md); `template.FuncMap` stays undefined under both bindings (see the text/template entry).
- [x] **`maps.DeleteFunc` is unbound**: it traps `undefined: maps.DeleteFunc` (go deletes the matching entries; `maps.EqualFunc` is in the host-twin unbound-members entry). Found fixing named-basic map keys.
- [x] **fmt orders composite map keys by their rendered text**: `fmt.Println(map[[2]B]int{{true, false}: 1, {false, true}: 2})` with `type B bool` whose `String` maps true to "a" and false to "z" prints `map[[a z]:1 [z a]:2]` where go prints `map[[z a]:2 [a z]:1]`; struct keys (`map[struct{X B}]int`) likewise. gc's fmtsort compares arrays and structs element by element on the underlying values; the `*runtime.Map` render (`intrinsics.go`) falls back to comparing rendered keys for anything `lessScript` cannot order. Also wrong on main. Found reviewing #1021.
- fmt orders composite map keys by their rendered text > fixed: the map-key sort compares composite keys element by element (`fmtsortKeyElems` on Slice.Elems / Struct.Fields) before the rendered-text fallback — pinned by `difffuzz/fmt_mapkey_composite`.
- [x] **NaN-keyed map entries print `NaN:<nil>`**: `fmt.Println(map[float64]int{math.NaN(): 1, 1: 3})` prints `map[NaN:<nil> 1:3]` (go `map[NaN:1 1:3]`): the render looks each sorted key up with `Map.Get`, which never finds a NaN key; it should read the stored value (`Map.At`). Also wrong on main. Found reviewing #1021.
- NaN-keyed map entries print `NaN:<nil>` > fixed: the map render sorts an index permutation over `SnapshotKeys` and reads values by `LookupCanonical`, so NaN keys print their stored values — pinned by `difffuzz/fmt_mapkey_nan`.
- [x] **`encoding/gob` traps at init**: `gob.NewEncoder(&buf).Encode(map[string]int{"a": 1})` traps `slice literal index *runtime.GoValue` in `gob.__init__` (`encoding/gob/decode.go`). Found reviewing #1021.
- `encoding/gob` traps at init > fixed: `litKeyIndex` reads a boxed host-scalar index (`reflect.Bool` in `decOpTable`) via `HostInt64` — pinned by `difffuzz/gob_roundtrip`.
- [x] **a multi-value call is accepted as one argument among others**: `fmt.Println("x", slices.BinarySearch([]int{1, 3}, 3))` prints `x 1 true` where gc rejects `multiple-value ... in single-value context` (a lone `f(g())` is fine). Found reviewing #1021.
- a multi-value call is accepted as one argument among others > fixed: compile emits `OpRejectTuple` after each non-lone `CallExpr` arg; a `*runtime.Tuple` on top of the stack traps `multiple-value call in single-value context` — pinned by `testdata/difffuzz/multivalue_arg/`
- [x] **a multi-value call leaks into other single-value contexts**: `[]any{0, g()}`, `x, y := g(), 3`, `map[string]any{"k": g()}` and `s[g()]` accept or mis-trap where gc rejects `multiple-value g() ... in single-value context`. Only non-lone call arguments are covered by `OpRejectTuple`. PENDING-pinned by `testdata/difffuzz/multivalue_ctx/`
- a multi-value call leaks into other single-value contexts > fixed: `OpRejectTuple` is emitted at the end of every `call()` emission instead — single-value contexts (literal elements, multi-RHS, index operands, binary operands, `return f(), x`) all trap; the legitimate tuple consumers (`f(g())`, `return g()`, `x, y := g()`, expression statements) arm `spreadTuple` — pinned by `testdata/difffuzz/multivalue_ctx/`
- [x] **perf: arithmetic on named-int values is slower than on plain ints** — `namedIntOp` fast path in `binaryOpFlags` (vm/binop.go): `Named{int64}` vs `UConst`/same-tag reads payloads, runs `intBinOp`, re-tags once; shifts/bool ops fall through (sum loop ~1.87s -> ~1.6s): summing a 1M-element `[]K` (`type K int`, `sk += ks[i] + 1`) takes ~187ms vs ~157ms for `[]int` (median of 3; a reviewer saw 64ms vs 44ms on another loop). Each op unwraps and re-tags the `Named`; a fast path in `binaryOpFlags` (`vm/vm.go`) for `Named{int64}` with a `UConst` or same-tag operand would cover it generally. Found reviewing #1021.
- [x] **`hash/maphash` hangs under interpretation** (found running [gosimilarity](https://github.com/Songmu/gosimilarity) under minigo): `maphash.MakeSeed()` loops forever because `randUint64` calls `runtime_rand`, a bodiless `//go:linkname runtime_rand runtime.rand` decl that `Hooks.Linkname` cannot resolve, so its zero-return shim keeps answering 0 to `for { if s != 0 { break } }`. Bind `runtime.rand` (or the maphash seed/hash leaves) — see the zero-return shim entry for the general fix. ([docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md](./docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md) §3.2)
- [x] **`weak` silently returns empty pointers** (met chasing the flagx gap running [gosimilarity](https://github.com/Songmu/gosimilarity) under minigo): `weak` is interpreted from GOROOT source and its `runtime_registerWeakPointer` linkname hits the zero-return shim, so `weak.Make(p).Value()` is nil where go returns `p`. The real `flagx@v0.0.2` happens to run anyway (it only uses `weak.Pointer` as a map key, so every FlagSet collides on one key — harmless with one FlagSet); its `--help` then trips the `reflect.Type.Size` gap. Bind `weak.Make`/`Pointer.Value` to strong pointers (no GC to observe) or let the shim trap. ([docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md](./docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md) §3.2)
- [x] **bodiless decls with an unresolvable `//go:linkname` silently return zero values** (`Hooks.Linkname` falls back to the zero-return shim): `//go:linkname rnd runtime.rand; func rnd() uint64` returns 0, which turned `maphash.MakeSeed` into an infinite loop and `weak.Make` into a nil pointer. Trap instead (minigo's contract is to fail loudly rather than answer wrong), keeping the shim only for decls known to be result-free no-ops; bind the few leaves that real code needs. ([docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md](./docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md) §3.2)
- [x] **`reflect.Type.Size` is missing on `minireflect.RType`**: `reflect.TypeOf(&x).Elem().Size()` traps `no member Size on host value *minireflect.RType`; flagx's usage printer calls it, so `gosimilarity --help` fails under minigo. ([docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md](./docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md) §3.3)
- `reflect.Type.Size` is missing on `minireflect.RType` > fixed: `RType.Size` reports the host layout for a host-backed type and the amd64 estimate `sizeOf` computes for a script type (difffuzz `reflect_type_size`).
- [x] **`os.IsPathSeparator` is unbound** — bound via `h.Fn1` + `IntOf` in internal/stdlib/os/os.go; difffuzz pin `os_ispathsep`: `os.IsPathSeparator('/')` traps `undefined`; gosimilarity reaches it in `canonicalPath` when a named path does not exist, so a typo'd argument traps instead of reporting an error. ([docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md](./docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md) §3.3)
- [x] **host in-place writes to script map args are lost** — `mapWriteBack` (internal/hostbridge/callhost.go) diffs the callee's map copy against the script map: inserts/rewrites apply, absent keys delete, nested maps recurse; also applied to untouched map elements inside slice args: a bound host func taking `map[K]V` (or `[]map[K]V`) gets a projected copy, so `m["k"] = v` / `rows[1]["k"] = v` inside it never reaches the caller (go sees it). Replacing an element (`rows[0] = newMap`) does write back since #1031. Fix direction: after the call, diff each untouched host map copy against the script map and apply the changes. ([docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md](./docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-reverify.md) §6.6)
- [x] **`reflect.Value.Convert(string → []byte)` builds a different `[]byte` than `[]byte(s)`** — `runtime.BytesOf` (runtime/strbytes.go) is the one shared shape; minireflect strToBytes + unsafe unsafeBytes delegate; difffuzz pin `reflect_strbytes`: `strToBytes` (minireflect) makes raw `int64` elements with its own typedef, while `unsafeBytes` (#951) uses `runtime.ByteValue` and the shared typedef, so `reflect.DeepEqual(reflect.ValueOf("hi").Convert(reflect.TypeOf([]byte(nil))).Interface(), []byte("hi"))` is `false` (go `true`). Share one host-`[]byte`→script helper and pin a difffuzz case. ([docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-refactoring.md](./docs/sketch/ja/experiment-gosimilarity-refactoring.md))
- [x] **minireflect `reflect.MakeFunc` does not pack variadic args**: a MakeFunc'd `func(string, ...int) string` called as `fn("a")` trapped `index out of range [1] with length 1` in the callback (go passes the variadic tail as one `[]int` Value: `2 slice 0`); `fn("a", 1, 2)`, `xs...` spreads, `Value.Call` and `Value.CallSlice` all handed over flat args.
- [x] **`reflect.ValueOf(fn).Call` on a MakeFunc'd variadic func passes a nil tail for zero extras**: once the func value went through an `any` (`reflect.ValueOf(fn)` with `var fn func(string, ...int) string`), its signature is no longer known to `Value.Call`, so `Call([]reflect.Value{ValueOf("a")})` hands the callback a nil `[]int` where go hands a non-nil empty one (`args[1].IsNil()` is `true`, go `false`). Direct calls and `Call` on the `MakeFunc` result itself are right.
- [x] **the older `slices` intrinsics trap on a nil slice**: `slices.IndexFunc(nilInts, f)` trapped `first arg must be a slice` where go returns `-1`; `EqualFunc`, `BinarySearch`, `BinarySearchFunc`, `Reverse`, `Insert`, `Delete`, `Repeat` and `maps.Copy` read their argument the same way. They now all go through `sliceParam` (nil, named nil, nil in a field/`any`/generic parameter), `Concat` is typed and nil-aware, and `Delete`'s vacated tail holds zeros instead of holes. Pinned by `testdata/difffuzz/slices_nil_args`. Same root as the `expvar.Map.Set` entry.
- [x] **explicit instantiation of a bound `slices`/`maps` function traps**: `f := slices.Contains[[]int]` traps `index on *runtime.BuiltinFunc` (go `true` for `f([]int{1}, 1)`); the same for `slices.Equal[[]int]`, `slices.Compare[[]int]` and the rest, so they cannot be passed as `cmp` callbacks (`slices.MaxFunc(grid, slices.Compare[[]int])`). `cmp.Compare[int]` works.
- [x] **`append` growth shares struct elements with the old backing array**: `a := []Rec{{"a", 1}}; b := append(a, Rec{"b", 2}); b[0].Name = "X"` also changes `a[0]` (go: the grown array holds copies, `a` stays `[{a 1}]`). The host `append` over `[]runtime.Value` copies the `*Struct` pointers; `slices.Insert`, `Grow` and `AppendSeq` that reallocate share it. Found while auditing the `slices` nil-argument fix.
- [x] **`%T` of a recovered `runtime.plainError` prints `runtime.PlainError`**: `recover()` from `m["a"] = 1` on a nil map, `close(nilChan)` or `maps.Copy(nilMap, nonEmpty)` formats as `runtime.PlainError` under `%T` (go: `runtime.plainError`); the host Go type's own name leaks through. Found while pinning `maps.Copy` on a nil map. Fixed: `ScriptTypeString` spells a `runtime.PlainError` payload `runtime.plainError`; difffuzz `fmt_plainerror_typename`.
- [x] **generic inference fails on a bare funclit argument to `iter.Pull`**: `iter.Pull(func(yield func(int) bool) { ... })` traps `undefined: V` at the call where go binds V=int from `Seq[V]`'s underlying func type (an explicit `iter.Seq[int](...)` conversion works). Found pinning `iter.Pull`. Fixed: `UnifyIndices` falls back to unifying the index args through the base type's underlying type expression (`Seq[V]` spells `func(func(V) bool)`) when the arg carries no instantiation binds — difffuzz `iter_pull_funclit`.
- [x] **`TestScriptProfiler`'s "deferred host call" check is timing-flaky on loaded CI**: `defer time.Sleep(150ms)` should charge ~150ms flat to `main.slowDefer`, but the assert `>= 100ms` landed 80ms (#1099) and 95ms (#1103) on CI while identical code passed on #1098 and locally. Raise the sleep (e.g. 300ms) or lower the assert; also confirm the coroutine-park registration (#1098) does not classify a deferred host call as parked time that the sampler then excludes. Stale: the sleep was raised to 400ms (e609b9b8) and `parkingHostMethods` (vm/sched.go) only registers Wait/Lock-family methods — a deferred `time.Sleep` never parks, so the sampler charges it to the calling frame as intended.
- [x] **a package-declared type name resolves in the use-site scope for shapes not covered by `declTypFile`**: `type Box int64` + `type S struct{ F Box }` at package level, then `unsafe.Sizeof(s.F)` inside a block that redeclares `type Box int8` prints 1 (go 8); likewise `var a, _ = pair()` with `func pair() (Box, int)` gives `unsafe.Sizeof(a)` 1 in the same block (go 8). #1109 pins the decl's file only on `binding.declTypFile`; struct field types and multi-result call inference still record a bare `ast.Expr`, so `typeIdent` re-resolves the name against the use site and finds the local shadow. Found in the #1109 review. Stale: re-verified — both shapes print 8 matching gc; the use-site resolution now lands on the package decl (shadow probe all-8s).
- [x] **Story preview: serve the app's static assets at their original paths**: the purpose of `examples/storybook` is the classic-MPA counterpart of how Storybook is used for SPAs — adjusting CSS and layout by rendering server templates in a real browser without real data — but the samples are self-contained HTML and `cmd/storybook` serves no assets, so a page referencing `/static/app.css` renders unstyled. Let the preview server mount the app's static directory at the same URL paths, give the example a real external stylesheet, and make a CSS-only edit visible on browser reload without re-running Go. See [the purpose review](./docs/sketch/ja/experiment-storybook.md).
- [x] **Story preview: compare data-state variants side by side**: CSS edits affect several data states at once (empty/one/many items, long names and unbreakable words, missing optional fields, no image, mixed scripts), but the preview shows one Story per page. The `?w=` viewport switcher landed with the real-app backport ([#1069](https://github.com/podhmo/minigo/pull/1069)); the index now groups stories by the render function they drive and each group header opens `/grid/<func>` — that template's data-state variants side by side at the chosen width (exported too as `grid_<func>.html`). The CSS live-reload loop itself was already recorded in the #1069 exercise. See [the purpose review](./docs/sketch/ja/experiment-storybook.md).
- [x] **Story preview: make render functions the primary Story target**: in Storybook terms the component is the page's render function (view struct → HTML), not the handler that also fetches data. Reorganize `examples/storybook` so data-state Stories call render functions with view structs (no fake Store/request/recorder), keep handler Stories as the fallback for code without a separated render function, and say so in the README. The value is in-browser CSS/layout adjustment, not `TestRenderXXX`-style HTML assertions. See [the purpose review](./docs/sketch/ja/experiment-storybook.md).
- [x] **Story preview: pick up live reload in consumers' copied helpers** ([#1079](https://github.com/podhmo/minigo/issues/1079)): `storybook.Book` grew the opt-in machinery (`Watch`, `MountLiveReload`, `ScriptTag`, `AppendLiveReload`) and `examples/storybook` exposes it as `-watch`; consumers carrying a copy of the helper — go-classic-mpa-sample's `cmd/storybook` — get it by syncing the copy and passing the flag.
- [x] **`Engine.Bind` should accept or resolve the declared package name** (#1068): `e.Bind("github.com/yuin/goldmark-alert", ...)` registers the package as `goldmark-alert` while scripts refer to it as `alert` → `undefined: alert`; the workaround is `e.BoundOf(path).Name = "alert"` after Bind. Take the name as an option or resolve it automatically.
- [x] **bound host struct literals are opaque to bound templates** (#1068): `store.Issue{...}` in a story produces script-side structs that bound `html/template` reflection cannot see, so `{{.Issue.ID}}` fails `can't evaluate field ID in type interface {}`. Either document the boundary (value-producing packages must stay source-interpreted) or add an API for real host struct literals.
- [x] **script-side `fs.FS` cannot cross into bound host code** (#1068): `//go:embed` yields a script `embed.FS` that failed on a bound host function's `fs.FS` parameter, and bound `html/template`/`text/template` lacked `ParseFS`; workaround was `fs.ReadFile` + `t.Parse(string(src))`. `adaptIface` now tries a `scriptFS` proxy when a script value declares `Open` — `ReadDir`/`ReadFile` variants surface only when declared, files carry `fs.File`+`ReadDir`/`FileInfo`/`DirEntry` proxies — and both bound template packages expose `ParseFS` (`vm/vm.go`, `testdata/scriptfs`). `fs.GlobFS`/`fs.StatFS` stay unadopted (host helpers fall back through `Open`), as do `fs.SubFS` and the minireflect `toHost` marshal path.
- [x] **helper to bind a whole package** (#1068): `Engine.Bind` enumerates each symbol in a `map[string]runtime.Value` by hand (goldmark needed 8 symbols across 4 packages). Provide a whole-package bind helper or at least document the pattern.
- [x] **incremental source-package caching for session-fork previews** (#1068): `NewSession()` keeps binds warm but re-parses the source package per request (~100-200 ms/req); fine at example scale, but file-level invalidation or a shared incremental cache would matter on larger apps. Sessions now share the base engine's `*token.FileSet`, a `parseCache` (path → `*syntax.File`, revalidated by sha256 of the bytes on every load so an edited file is re-parsed), and an `indexCache` (file-pointer fingerprint → `index.Index`, bounded); `BenchmarkSessionForkReload` went 5.55 ms → 1.88 ms Package-only and 6.92 ms → 1.90 ms including the call. Remaining per-fork cost is `LocateDir` (~0.7 ms) and `NewSession` itself (~0.9 ms: builtins + intrinsics) — the next lever if preview latency still matters.
- [x] **missing field in bound `html/template` renders empty instead of erroring** (#1068): `{{.Nonexistent}}` on a script-side struct produces empty output where gc errors `can't evaluate field Nonexistent in type ...`; a typo'd or renamed field is undetectable. Match the stdlib error or add a strict mode — the main diagnostics blind spot for template-heavy use.
- [x] **story discovery could be a shared helper package** (#1068): the build-tag → story-func scan (now two signatures: print and `func(http.ResponseWriter, *http.Request)` via the `StoryHttp` prefix) is reimplemented per consumer; a small shared `storybook` package outside examples would serve real apps.
- [x] **`mime/multipart.NewWriter` traps at `crypto/rand` init** (found reproducing [#1084](https://github.com/podhmo/minigo/issues/1084)): `multipart.NewWriter`/`rand.Text` reached `crypto/rand` → `internal/syscall/unix`, which trapped on missing `syscall` symbols (`SYS_UNLINKAT`, later `ELOOP`). `crypto/rand` is now bound (Reader/Read/Text/Int/Prime) so the writer's `randomBoundary` stays host-side; `io.SectionReader` is a bound `hostType` too, so direct promoted calls on `multipart.sectionReadCloser` resolve. Pinned by `testdata/difffuzz/multipart_writer`; `FileHeader.Open` still traps on the host-embed interface-satisfaction gap (`testdata/difffuzz/multipart_fileheader_open` PENDING).
- [x] **a type-only member touch runs the package initializer** (found pinning M0-4 in `lazypin_test.go`): `var t depb.T` — and host-side `pkg.Member("T")` — initializes `depb` even though only the type's shape is needed, so a panicking `init()`/`var` initializer surfaces on a pure type query. Host-side index queries (`Engine.Package` + `pkg.Index`) correctly stay at `Indexed`. The rearchitecture's `IndexedOnly`/`AllowIndex` sites should answer type members without initializing; until then the eager init is pinned as current behavior in `TestLazyInitPin`. → already landed: `TestLazyInitPin/{TypeMemberTouchStaysLazy,ScriptTypeMemberTouchStaysLazy}` pins the lazy type-member path green.
- [x] **a local variable shadowing an import alias still dispatches the registered special form** (found pinning M0-6 in `quotepin_test.go` `LocalShadowingStillDispatches`): `import d "example.com/define"; d := localT{}; d.Convert(5)` fires `OpSpecialCall` — `trySpecial` resolves the callee via `importRef(id.Name)` (the file's import table) without checking `c.fs` locals first, so the method call on the local is hijacked and returns the special's result instead of 50. Fix: `trySpecial` should skip dispatch when `id.Name` is bound in local scope (same shadowing rule the normal identifier path already follows).
- [x] **strict lazy: untyped bound-package const overflows in a shift count** (found by `TestStrictLazyConformance`, `testdata/difffuzz/text_pass_constdom` STRICT_PENDING): `x << (math.MaxUint + 0)` traps `constant 18446744073709551615 overflows int` under `WithStrictLazyCompile`; the deferred untyped const is coerced through `int` before the count clamps to uint. Normal mode and gc print 0. → already landed: `testdata/difffuzz/text_pass_constdom` carries no STRICT_PENDING marker and converges under `WithStrictLazyCompile`.
- [x] **strict lazy: bound-package member compare resolves a bogus import path** (found by `TestStrictLazyConformance`, `testdata/difffuzz/time_weekday_bound` STRICT_PENDING): `d == time.Friday` traps `resolve ".imp:time": import path ".imp:time" could not be resolved` under `WithStrictLazyCompile` — a deferred gate/typedef path fabricates an `.imp:` path instead of answering from the bound package's own typedef. → already landed: `testdata/difffuzz/time_weekday_bound` carries no STRICT_PENDING marker and converges under `WithStrictLazyCompile`.
- [x] **`x.(**T)` on a bound host type asserts false where gc asserts true** (found pinning M4-4): a host `**probeT` value asserted to `**lib.T` (`lib` bound via `HostTypeOf`) returns `false` — the unnamed-pointer fallback in `vm/assert.go` compares the spelled `*lib.T` (the bound import path) with the minted `*<go pkgpath>.T`, which never agree. Either twin `*lib.T`'s element through the bound typedef's host backing, or spell asserts by Go pkgpath when the elem resolves a bound twin. Pinned as current behavior in `TestBoundStarStarAssert` (`testdata/starptr`).
- [x] **a FuncMap func declaring a non-empty interface param rejects a named host map/slice arg** (found in the #1372 review): `func(e interface{ Encode() string })` given `url.Values` traps `wrong type for value; expected interface { Encode() string }; got map[string][]string` — the arg arrives as the unboxed host map shape and `Lift`'s coerce checks the interface against it, not the named `url.Values` (gc calls `Encode`). Pinned by `testdata/difffuzz/template_funcmap_hostiface`.
- [x] **`fmt` invokes the underlying host type's `String()` on a declared-over-host value** (found fixing `MyBuf(b)` conversions): a `type MyBuf bytes.Buffer` value prints `hi` for `%v` — fmt dispatches `Stringer` through the underlying `*bytes.Buffer` box — where gc prints the struct dump `{[104 105] 0 0}` because `MyBuf` does not inherit `bytes.Buffer`'s methods. Same divergence applies to any declared type over a host struct with a `String()`/`Error()`/`Marshal*` method. → landed: `MethodInfoOfValue` routes a script-declared typedef (File set, or an anonymous `*T` over one) to its own method walk instead of the host reflect set, and `fmt` renders the native payload field-wise (`hostFields`), so `%v`/`%+v`/`%#v`/`%s`/`%q` and `&b` all match gc — pinned by `difffuzz/fmt_declared_stringer`.
- [x] **`fmt.Errorf` `*` operand on a reflect.Value reads its inner int** (found in the #1368 review rounds): `Errorf("%*.*s", "x", reflect.ValueOf(2), "y")` prints `%!(BADWIDTH)y` (go: `%!(BADWIDTH)%!(BADPREC)y`) — the marshalled arg loses the reflect.Value box, so the host `*` accepts `2` where gc's intFromArg rejects reflect.Value. The %w rewrite pins the same markers on its own path; the bare (no-%w) path never sees them. Pinned by `testdata/difffuzz/fmt_errorf_star_rvalue`.
- [x] **`os.Symlink` is not bound**: a script calling it traps `undefined: os.Symlink` (go: creates the link). Found pinning the fsPath difffuzz case — the WalkDir("lnk/")-descends check needs a real symlink. Fixed: bound `os.Symlink` + `os.Readlink`; the link's target text is stored verbatim (resolving it wrote the absolute path in), and `*os.LinkError` paths rewrite to the script spelling. difffuzz `os_symlink_readlink`.
- [x] **the bound `time.Location` zero value is `time.Local`, not `Location{}`** (found probing `&x` on bound struct returns): `var loc time.Location; loc.String()` prints `"Local"` where gc prints `""`, and `&loc == time.Local` is true (go: false) — the `HostType` constructor returned `stdtime.Local`. `new(time.Location)` diverged the same way. Fixed: the constructor boxes `&stdtime.Location{}` like every other bound struct. difffuzz `location_zero`.
- [x] **promoted members of embedded structs are unreachable through `interface{}` values** ([#1228](https://github.com/podhmo/minigo/issues/1228)): a `*Comment` in a `map[string]any` answered `{{.C.Moderation.Hidden}}` but trapped `can't evaluate field Hidden in type interface {}` on the promoted spelling — the `structDataHost` projection materialized only declared members. Fixed: `hostbridge/promoted.go` walks embeds gc's way (shallowest depth wins, same-depth ties stay out, value-vs-pointer method sets, defined-type and host-typed embeds included) and adds the promoted fields and methods to the projection. Pinned by `testdata/difffuzz/template_embed_promoted`. Residual: a promoted member reached only through a nil embedded pointer reports can't-evaluate where gc errors "indirection through nil pointer to embedded struct field T"; interface-typed embeds stay leaves.
- [x] **repl completion tests fail locally on a clean `main` while CI is green** ([#1393](https://github.com/podhmo/minigo/issues/1393)): `go test ./repl` fails `TestCompleteClosedImportLiteral` (open import literal loses its candidates), `TestCompleteImportPaths` (`strings` missing in `import "str"` candidates) and `TestCompleteCommandArgPackageRefs` (`io`/`encoding/json` missing) — environment-dependent, not a landed regression. Investigate what the environment must provide (module cache? GOPATH layout?) or document the requirement. Fixed: distro Go splits GOROOT (`/usr/lib/go-1.26/src` is a symlink to `../../share/go-1.26/src`) and `filepath.WalkDir` Lstats its root so it never descended; `importables()` now resolves walk roots through `filepath.EvalSymlinks` (`resolveWalkRoot`, applied to the GOROOT/src and module-root walks).
